Method: 采用粒子力学方法（一阶/二阶自旋粒子模型），让无质量粒子在余维二“刺猬”膜世界背景中沿紧致角向额外维运动，进行正则量子化，动态求解有效势与卡鲁扎-克莱因谱；进一步引入 N=1、2 自旋粒子模型，分析自旋-曲率耦合对有效势的修正及能级依赖的局域化行为。

Result: 玻色扇区不产生径向束缚；自旋-曲率耦合修改有效势并产生依赖能级的卡鲁扎-克莱因模式局域化图像，形成动力学确定的质量谱。

Conclusion: 在通量支撑的余维二膜世界中，几何质量生成可通过粒子力学实现，体几何、粒子自旋与四维有效质量谱之间存在紧密关联。

Abstract: We investigate a geometric mechanism for mass generation in a codimension-two hedgehog braneworld. A massless particle propagating in the bulk acquires an effective four-dimensional mass through its motion along the compact angular extra dimension, yielding a dynamically determined Kaluza-Klein spectrum after canonical quantization. While the bosonic sector exhibits no radial confinement, extending the analysis to N = 1, 2 spinning-particle models shows that the spin-curvature coupling modifies the effective potential and introduces a level-dependent localization pattern for the Kaluza-Klein modes. The results establish a particle-mechanics realization of geometric mass generation in flux-supported codimension-two braneworlds and highlight the interplay between bulk geometry, spin, and the effective four-dimensional mass spectrum.

</details>


### [100] [Splitting Dynamics of Multiply Quantized Vortices in Holographic Superfluid of Finite Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08831)
*Shanquan Lan,Xin Li,Jiexiong Mo,Yu Tian,Yu-Kun Yan,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维全息超流体中卷绕数 n=5~8 的多量子化涡旋在有限温度下的分裂动力学，采用准正规模线性微扰分析与完全非线性实时数值模拟相结合的方法，揭示了三个新物理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对多量子化涡旋不稳定性的研究多基于零温或低卷绕数情形，不稳定模式数目遵循 2n-3 公式。作者希望探究高卷绕数（n=5~8）与有限温度下该规律是否仍然成立，以及主导不稳定模式随温度的转变和分裂拓扑结构如何演化。

Method: 以二维全息超流为模型，在有限温度下对卷绕数 n=5,6,7,8 的多量子化涡旋进行两类分析：一是准正规模（quasinormal modes）的线性微扰分析，确定各不稳定模式的增长率和模式标号 p；二是完全非线性的实时数值模拟，验证线性分析预测的分裂动力学与最终拓扑结构。

Result: 揭示三个新现象：(1) 随 n 增大，不稳定模式数目不再严格遵循 2n-3 公式；n=8 涡旋中 p=2(n-1) 的不稳定模式在整个温度范围内缺失，仅存在 2n-4 个不稳定模式。(2) 主导不稳定模式随温度升高的转变呈现新特征：n≤6 时主导模式依次经历 p=2,3,…,n；而 n≥7 时出现跳跃式转变，如 n=7 在 T=0.325T_c 从 p=2 跳到 p=4，再在 T=0.359T_c 直接跳到 p=7；n=8 则在 T=0.302T_c 直接从 p=2 跳到 p=8。(3) 高卷绕数涡旋的单一分裂模式可包含多个具有不同拓扑结构的子分裂模式，如 n=8 涡旋的 l=4 模式在低、中、高温度下呈现三种子模式。非线性模拟证实了线性稳定性分析的预测。

Conclusion: 高卷绕数多量子化涡旋在有限温度下的分裂动力学突破了传统的 2n-3 不稳定模式计数规律，主导模式随温度出现跳跃式转变，且单一分裂模式可蕴含多种拓扑子模式。这些结果对冷原子实验中的涡旋分裂观测具有指导意义。

Abstract: We study the splitting dynamics of multiply quantized vortices with winding numbers $n=5,6,7$ and $8$ in a two-dimensional holographic superfluid at finite temperature, by combining linear perturbation analysis of quasinormal modes with fully nonlinear real-time numerical simulations. Three new physical phenomena are revealed. First, the number of unstable modes no longer strictly follows the $2n-3$ formula as $n$ increases. For the vortex with $n=8$, the unstable mode with $p=2(n-1)$ is absent throughout the entire temperature range, so that only $2n-4$ unstable modes exist. Second, the transition of the dominant unstable mode with increasing temperature exhibits new characteristics. For vortices with $n\le 6$, the dominant mode changes sequentially as $p=2,3,\dots,n$, whereas for $n\ge 7$ jump-like transitions occur-for instance, for $n=7$ the dominant mode jumps from $p=2$ to $p=4$ at $T=0.325T_c$ and then directly to $p=7$ at $T=0.359T_c$, and for $n=8$ it jumps directly from $p=2$ to $p=8$ at $T=0.302T_c$. Third, a single splitting pattern of high-winding-number vortices can contain multiple sub-splitting patterns with distinct topological structures, as exemplified by the $l=4$ pattern of the $n=8$ vortex, which exhibits three sub-patterns at low, intermediate and high temperatures. The nonlinear simulations confirm the predictions of the linear stability analysis, and the implications of our results for cold-atom experiments are discussed.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum spacetime and gravity from the IKKT matrix model: an invitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08955)
*Harold C. Steinacker*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一时空、引力与基础物理，提出一种基于IKKT矩阵模型的框架，利用矩阵模型背景自发生成时空，从而为引力和量子引力提供非标准描述，并解释宇宙膨胀等结构。

Method: 以IKKT矩阵模型的非平凡真空/矩阵背景作为3+1维非交换时空；讨论一类描述膨胀且均匀各向同性FLRW时空的背景，它们是一圈有效作用量的解（每体积具有有限自由度）；对背景做扰动得到在时空上传播的场，由弱耦合规范理论支配，并给出非标准的（量子）引力描述。

Result: 得到了满足一圈有效作用量方程、每体积有限自由度的FLRW背景解；扰动产生弱耦合规范场及带红外修正的非标准引力描述；最大超对称性确保有效局域性。

Conclusion: 基于IKKT矩阵模型的框架能自然产生3+1维非交换时空，支持描述膨胀FLRW宇宙背景，并通过最大超对称确保有效局域性，为引力和基础物理提供了结构自洽的出发点。

Abstract: This is a concise and compact introduction to a framework for spacetime, gravity, and fundamental physics based on the IKKT matrix model, focusing on conceptual and structural aspects. The approach rests on nontrivial vacua or matrix backgrounds, which serve as 3+1-dimensional noncommutative spacetime. We discuss a specific class of backgrounds describing expanding homogeneous and isotropic FLRW spacetime, which provide a solution of the one-loop effective action with finitely many dof per volume. Perturbations of the background give rise to fields propagating on spacetime governed by a weakly-coupled gauge theory, as well as a nonstandard description of (quantum) gravity leading to some IR modifications. Maximal supersymmetry of the model is essential to ensure effective locality.

</details>


### [102] [5d SCFT fixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09007)
*Michele Del Zotto,Andrea Sangiovanni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了一类新的 5d SCFT 构造单元——“fixtures”，用于填补具有非单连通(non-simply laced)全局对称性的 5d SCFT 原子分类中的空缺，并可通过 M 理论奇异三重的几何实现来构造非单连通味对称性的 5d 共形物质理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 5d SCFT 的原子分类框架中，缺乏具有非单连通全局对称性的构造单元(building blocks)，限制了完整分类的推进。作者旨在通过引入新的一类 5d UV 固定点(即“fixtures”)来填补这一空白，从而实现对非单连通味对称性 5d 共形物质理论的构造。

Method: 作者提出 5d SCFT“fixtures”作为新的构造单元，将其理解为 Higgs 分支 RG 流的产物；在几何上，通过显式识别 M 理论中的奇异三重(singular threefolds)来实现这些固定点。借助该方法，可以系统构造具有非单连通味对称性的 5d 共形物质 SCFT，并进一步研究其圆约化(circle reduction)与 4d class-S 型理论的关系。

Result: 成功引入并显式几何实现了 5d SCFT "fixtures" 这一类新的 UV 固定点，填补了非单连通全局对称性构造单元的空白；并由此构造出具有非单连通味对称性的 5d 共形物质 SCFT，同时厘清了其圆约化与 4d class-S 型理论的对应关系。

Conclusion: 通过引入 5d SCFT "fixtures"，作者为 5d SCFT 原子分类提供了缺失的非单连通全局对称性构造单元，拓展了 5d 共形物质理论的构造空间，并建立了与 4d class-S 理论在圆约化下的联系，完善了 5d SCFT 的分类拼图。

Abstract: In the context of the atomic classification of 5d SCFTs building blocks with non-simply laced global symmetries are lacking. In this work we fill this gap by introducing a new class of 5d UV fixed points (the 5d SCFT "fixtures"). Physically, 5d SCFTs fixtures are the result of a Higgs branch RG flow, realized geometrically by M-theory on singular threefolds which we identify explicitly. In particular, this enables the construction of 5d conformal matter SCFTs with non-simply laced flavor symmetry. Along the way, we discuss their circle reduction in relation to 4d theories of class-S type.

</details>


### [103] [M5 Instantons in ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09041)
*Jieming Lin,Jesse van Muiden,Zihan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究M理论渐近AdS₄×S⁷/Z_k几何中（ABJM对偶理论）超对称M5膜瞬子的存在性，发现其超对称嵌入由κ对称性决定并局域化到不动点，但其单圈配分函数为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究渐近AdS₄×S⁷/Z_k的M理论背景中是否存在超对称M5膜瞬子，以与对偶ABJM理论的S³配分函数结果相对照，厘清体配分函数的正确展开方式。

Method: 利用M5膜的κ对称性确定超对称嵌入条件，证明瞬子必须局域化到渐近AdS₄几何中的等变不动点并环绕(S³×S³)/Z_k；在量子层面计算这些瞬子的单圈配分函数。

Result: 超对称M5膜瞬子被κ对称性强制局域化到等变不动点并环绕(S³×S³)/Z_k；其单圈配分函数为零，表明体配分函数应改用超引力模式和M2膜瞬子展开。

Conclusion: M5膜瞬子对配分函数无贡献，体配分函数应由超引力模式与M2膜瞬子展开，这与对偶ABJM理论S³配分函数的已知结果一致。

Abstract: We study the existence of supersymmetric M5-brane instantons in asymptotically AdS$_4 \times S^7/\mathbf{Z}_k$ geometries of M-theory, dual to the ABJM theory. The supersymmetric embeddings of these instantons are determined by the M5-brane $κ$-symmetry, which forces them to localise to the equivariant fixed points in the asymptotically AdS$_4$ geometry, and wrap $(S^3\times S^3)/\mathbf{Z}_k $. At the quantum level we find that the one-loop partition function of these instantons vanishes, suggesting that the bulk partition function is instead to be expanded in terms of supergravity modes and M2-brane instantons, in line with known results for the $S^3$ partition function of the dual ABJM theory.

</details>

### [104] [Superdiffusive two-dimensional superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05447)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文将仅含相位的Popov作用量推广为利用非局域分数阶算子的框架，通过将拉普拉斯算符替换为Riesz分数阶拉普拉斯 $-(-\nabla^2)^{\alpha/2}$（$1<\alpha<2$），证明涡旋-反涡旋相互作用由对数禁闭转变为幂律禁闭 $V(r)\propto r^{2-\alpha}$，从而在有限温度下抑制涡旋增殖并稳定真正的长程序，并据此构造了分数阶London方程以统一描述非局域电动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维超导体在传统局域理论（满足Mermin-Wagner限制）下难以在有限温度维持真正的长程序，涡旋增殖会破坏超导相干。作者希望引入非局域（分数阶）作用量，以突破Mermin-Wagner限制的适用范围，探索有限温度下实现真正长程序的可能性。

Method: 将仅含相位的Popov作用量推广到非局域框架，用Riesz分数阶拉普拉斯 $-(-\nabla^2)^{\alpha/2}$（$1<\alpha<2$）替换标准空间拉普拉斯算符，导出涡旋-反涡旋相互作用；通过推广的Kosterlitz-Thouless能量-熵分析证明热涡旋增殖被严格抑制；进一步构造规范不变的分数阶作用量并推导分数阶London方程，得到可映射到反常Pippard极限的实空间幂律核。

Result: 涡旋-反涡旋相互作用由对数禁闭转为幂律禁闭 $V(r)\propto r^{2-\alpha}$；该非局域禁闭严格抑制了热涡旋增殖，使系统脱离Mermin-Wagner限制的严格适用范围，从而在有限温度下天然稳定真正的长程序；分数阶London方程给出实空间幂律核，对应长相干长度超导体的反常Pippard极限。

Conclusion: 非局域分数阶框架通过将涡旋相互作用强化为幂律禁闭，抑制了Kosterlitz-Thouless类型的涡旋增殖，从而在二维有限温度下稳定真正的长程序，并为非局域电动力学（反常Pippard极限）提供了一个唯象统一的描述。

Abstract: We formulate a non-local, field-theoretic description of phase fluctuations and topological transitions in two-dimensional (2D) superconductors by generalizing the local only-phase Popov action to a framework utilizing non-local fractional operators. By replacing the standard spatial Laplacian with the Riesz fractional Laplacian $-(-\nabla^2)^{α/2}$ (where $1<α<2$), we prove that the vortex-antivortex interaction transitions from logarithmic confinement to a stronger power-law confinement of the form $V(r)\propto r^{2-α}$. Through a generalized Kosterlitz-Thouless energetic-entropic analysis, we demonstrate that this non-local confinement strictly suppresses thermal vortex proliferation. Because the non-local operator safely places the system outside the strict domain of validity of the Mermin-Wagner restriction, this power-law confinement natively stabilizes true long-range order at finite temperatures. Finally, by constructing a gauge-invariant fractional action, we formulate a fractional London equation. This approach yields a real-space power-law kernel that maps directly onto the anomalous Pippard limit of superconductors with long coherence lengths, providing a unified phenomenologically motivated framework for non-local electrodynamics.

</details>


### [105] [Pair density wave order from non-symmorphic momentum symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05603)
*Matteo Dürrnagel,C. Alexander Baum,Michael Klett,Lennart Klebl,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 作者构建了一类微观晶格模型，在渐近精确的弱耦合极限下自发涌现配对密度波（PDW）序，其机制源于电子投影空间群表示所隐含的非简单空间群动量对称性，并指出莫尔系统和扩展s波交变磁体可能是PDW的潜在宿主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对密度波（PDW）序在多种非常规超导体中受到关注，但其微观起源和稳定机制仍不清晰。作者旨在提供一个可从弱耦合渐近精确推导出PDW的微观机制，而非常见的唯象或强关联解释。

Method: 构建一类微观晶格模型，利用电子所处的投影空间群表示（projective space group representation）所蕴含的非简单空间群（non-symmorphic）动量对称性，在渐近精确的弱耦合极限下进行解析与微扰分析，推导并证明PDW序的自发涌现。

Result: 证明了在该类晶格模型中，非简单空间群动量对称性机制可导致PDW序在渐近精确的弱耦合极限下稳定出现，并进一步指出该机制表明莫尔（moiré）系统与扩展s波交变磁体（extended s-wave altermagnets）是PDW序的潜在宿主材料。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种从弱耦合极限严格可控地产生PDW的微观机制，丰富了PDW的理论图景，并为在莫尔系统和交变磁体中寻找PDW指明了材料方向。

Abstract: We develop a class of microscopic lattice models in which pair density wave order emerges in the asymptotically exact weak coupling limit. The underlying mechanism is due to non-symmorphic momentum symmetry implied by the models' projective space group representation of electrons. Our mechanism suggests moiré systems and extended s-wave altermagnets as potential hosts for pair density wave order.

</details>


### [106] [Extended strange metal regime in a pure nematic quantum critical superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05875)
*Kousuke Ishida,Kiyotaka Mukasa,Shusaku Imajo,Andrew Hardy,Mingwei Qiu,Mikihiko Saito,Aavishkar A. Patel,Kohei Matsuura,Yuichi Sugimura,Yu Uezono,Takumi Otsuka,Nigel E. Hussey,Takao Watanabe,Koichi Kindo,Takasada Shibauchi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究在非磁性 FeSe1-xTex 体系中，借助强脉冲磁场抑制超导，揭示了隐藏在超导穹顶下方的奇异金属基态，并发现其由纯电子向列性量子临界点（QCP）驱动，且临界区因无序导致的向列涨落空间随机性而被展宽（与 FeSe1-xSx 的量子临界扇形成对比）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温超导常源于奇异金属态，传统观点认为磁性临界涨落是奇异金属性与超导转变温度提升的来源。本文旨在检验非磁性体系中，纯电子向列性量子临界涨落是否能同样驱动奇异金属性并促进超导配对，以及无序在其中扮演的角色。

Method: 利用强脉冲磁场压制超导态，暴露出 FeSe1-xTex 在超导穹顶下方的基态输运行为；系统测量其电阻率随温度的变化，并与更"干净"的 FeSe1-xSx 体系（呈现向列 QCP 上方的量子临界扇）进行对比；同时结合混合量子蒙特卡洛（hybrid QMC）模拟，检验无序诱导的向列涨落空间随机性对临界区展宽的效应。

Result: FeSe1-xTex 中存在一个由纯电子向列性表征的量子临界点（自发旋转对称破缺）。其奇异金属态并非出现在奇异 QCP 上方的扇形区域，而是跨越很宽的组分范围；配对相互作用被非磁性向列临界涨落增强。与之对比，FeSe1-xSx 表现出标准的量子临界扇。模拟表明，无序诱导的向列涨落空间随机性很可能将 FeSe1-xTex 的 QCP 扩大为一个延展的临界区。

Conclusion: 超导性的增强源于纯向列临界涨落、奇异金属性与无序之间的独特协同相互作用，这为理解多种关联材料中非费米液体输运的出现提供了新见解。

Abstract: High-temperature superconductivity often emerges from a strange metallic state, where the electrical resistivity exhibits a linear-in-temperature dependence over an anomalously extended temperature range. The prevailing belief is that magnetic critical fluctuations gives rise to strange metallicity, enhancing the superconducting transition temperature. Here, using high pulsed magnetic fields, we have uncovered the strange metallic ground state hidden below the superconducting dome of nonmagnetic FeSe$_{1-x}$Te$_{x}$, which harbors a quantum critical point (QCP) of pure electronic nematicity, characterized by spontaneous rotational symmetry breaking. Unlike the conventional quantum criticality, this strange metallic state does not appear in a fan-shaped region above the singular QCP but spans a wide compositional range, where pairing interactions are strengthened by nonmagnetic nematic critical fluctuations. This stands in sharp contrast to the much cleaner system FeSe$_{1-x}$S$_{x}$, which displays a quantum critical fan above nematic QCP, indicating that disorder-induced spatial randomness of the nematic fluctuations likely enlarges the QCP of FeSe$_{1-x}$Te$_{x}$ into an extended region of criticality, as suggested by relevant hybrid Quantum Monte Carlo simulations. These observations highlight superconductivity promoted by a unique interplay between pure nematic critical fluctuations, strange metallicity and disorder, providing new insight into the emergence of non-Fermi-liquid transport in various correlated materials.

</details>


### [107] [Microscopic calculation of coherence lengths and magnetic penetration depth in multiband superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06014)
*Tristan Ryoma Fuchs,Takuya Nomoto,Hikaru Watanabe,Ryotaro Arita*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种扩展的 Ginzburg-Landau 方法，用于在远低于 Tc 的低温下计算超导相干长度和磁穿透深度，通过仅对协变梯度项做微扰展开并保留序参量的全部依赖，以较低计算成本获得了与 BdG 实空间计算一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Ginzburg-Landau 理论对自由能同时展开序参量和其梯度，仅在 Tc 附近有效，无法准确描述远低于 Tc 的低温行为。而实空间 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 计算虽准确但计算量大，因此需要一种兼顾精度与效率的框架，并能适用于更真实的（多带）电子结构。

Method: 作者提出扩展 GL 方法：不对序参量本身做展开，而是只对协变梯度项做微扰展开，保留自由能对超导序参量的完整依赖。协变梯度项的系数通过在小外加对动量下评估微观自由能的有限差分来确定。该方法适用于单带和多带超导体。

Result: 在所考察的模型上，扩展 GL 方法在很宽的温度范围内与实空间 BdG 计算吻合良好，同时所需计算量大幅降低。

Conclusion: 扩展 GL 方法为低温下计算超导相干长度与磁穿透深度提供了高效且准确的框架，并能纳入更真实的电子结构，是传统 GL 理论与全微观 BdG 计算之间的有效折中。

Abstract: We present an extended Ginzburg-Landau (GL) method for calculating the superconducting coherence length and magnetic penetration depth at temperatures well below the transition temperature $T_{\mathrm c}$. In contrast to conventional GL theory, which expands the free energy in both the order parameters and their gradients, our method applies a perturbative expansion only to the covariant-gradient terms, while retaining the full dependence on the superconducting order parameters. The coefficients of these terms are determined from finite differences of microscopic free energies evaluated at small imposed pair momenta. The method applies to both single-band and multiband superconductors and therefore provides a framework for incorporating more realistic electronic structures. For the models examined here, the extended GL method agrees well with real-space Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) calculations over a wide temperature range, while requiring substantially less computational effort.

</details>


### [108] [Effect of non-homogeneous non-magnetic Impurities in Superconductors: A comparison between Microscopic and Macroscopic theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06236)
*Carlos Redondo Herrero,Tejas Guruswamy,Orlando Quaranta,Akira Miyazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文从微观 Eilenberger 方程出发，研究超导体中非均匀非磁性杂质对表面电阻和过热场的影响，并与基于 London 方程的唯象方法对比，证明两套理论给出相同的电磁场分布，但在磁场相关量（Bean-Livingston 势垒 vs 过热场）上存在差异，最终构造出一类使过热场达到临界场的杂质分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来非均匀非磁性杂质被视作降低超导体表面电阻、提高过热场的有前景手段，但对其作用机制缺乏深入的微观理论理解，且唯象 London 方法在确定磁场分布时的有效性有待系统检验。

Method: 利用 Eilenberger 方程进行微观理论分析，并与基于 London 方程的唯象方法对比；对任意空间杂质分布求解两种理论下的电磁场分布，并基于微观形式推导过热场。

Result: 微观与宏观理论对超导体内部任意杂质空间分布均给出完全一致的电磁场分布；但宏观模型仅给出 Bean-Livingston 势垒，而微观理论给出与实验相关的过热场。基于微观形式，作者确定了一类杂质分布，使过热场 H_sh 等于临界场 H_c，即达到理论最大值。

Conclusion: 唯象 London 方法足以准确描述磁场分布，但无法正确给出过热场等关键物理量；微观 Eilenberger 理论则能精确确定过热场，并可据此设计杂质分布，使过热场达到临界场的理论上限。

Abstract: Non-homogeneous, non-magnetic impurities in superconductivity have recently gained traction as a promising approach to reduce surface resistance and increase the superheating field. We aim to deepen the understanding of impurities' role from a theoretical perspective by utilizing Eilenberger's equation, and to investigate the validity of a more phenomenological approach based on London's equation for determining the magnetic field profile within the superconductor. We show that the microscopic and macroscopic theories produce identical electromagnetic field distributions for any spatial distributions of impurities inside the superconductors. However, the two approaches provide different magnetic field-related quantities. The macroscopic model only gives the Bean-Livingston barrier, while the microscopic theory provides the experimentally-relevant superheating field. Based on the microscopic formalism, we determine a family of impurity profiles that make the superheating field $H_{\rm{sh}}$ equal to the critical field $H_c$, which is the maximum achievable.

</details>


### [109] [$^{139}$La nuclear quadrupole resonance studies of pressurized La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06453)
*Meng Zhang,Zhuo Wang,Yantao Cao,Yang Yuan,Kangjian Luo,Shanxiang Gao,Hanjie Guo,Yongkang Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用139La核四极共振系统研究La4Ni3O10中密度波序的性质及其在压力下的演化，发现自旋与电荷密度波相互交织，且短程与长程序在不同温度出现并对压力响应不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度波序被认为是与非常规超导竞争的序，普遍存在于多种超导体（包括新发现的Ruddlesden-Popper相镍氧化物）中。理解密度波序的性质及其与超导态的相互作用，对揭示非常规超导机制至关重要。

Method: 采用139La核四极共振（NQR）技术，系统研究La4Ni3O10中密度波序的性质；并通过施加2.3 GPa静水压，测量短程与长程密度波序转变温度的压力演化。

Result: 发现La4Ni3O10中自旋密度波与电荷密度波相互交织，与La3Ni2O7中的情形形成鲜明对比。短程密度波序在约150 K出现，远高于约139 K处形成的长程密度波序。施加2.3 GPa静水压后，短程与长程序的转变温度分别以1 K/GPa和10 K/GPa的速率下降。

Conclusion: 结果证实自旋密度波与电荷密度波均为La4Ni3O10中与超导态竞争的序，并为理解密度波序与非常规超导之间的相互作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: Density-wave (DW) orders are considered as competing orders to unconventional superconductivity and are commonly seen in a variety of superconductors including but not limited to the recently discovered Ruddlesden-Popper-phase nickelates. By utilizing $^{139}$La nuclear quadrupole resonance, we systematically investigate into the nature of DW orders and their evolution under pressure in La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$. Spin and charge DW orders are found to be intertwined in this material, which is in stark contrast to those in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$. Short-range DW orders are observed near 150 K, well above the development of long-range DW orders at around 139 K. Upon applying a hydrostatic pressure of 2.3 GPa, the transition temperatures of the short-range and long-range orders decrease at rates of 1 K/GPa and 10 K/GPa, respectively. Our results thus affirm that both spin density wave and charge density wave as competing orders with the superconducting state in La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$, and provide new insights into the interplay between DW orders and unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [110] [Discovery of Superconductivity in a Bulk Moire Superlattice Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06622)
*Subham Naik,Paul Monson,Susanta Manna,Prabuddhakant Mishra,Soumyojit Chatterjee,Sandip Kuila,Partha Pratim Jana,M. B. Sreedhara,Rahul Sharma,Gohil S. Thakur*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了在一种本征生长的块体莫尔晶体 (Sr6TaS8)1+x(TaS2)8 中，于常压下观测到体超导电性（Tc = 2.5 K），并在约 270 K 处发现疑似电荷密度波转变，证明超导是本征莫尔材料中的涌现相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔材料中的平带可增强关联效应，从而产生超导、铁磁、Mott 绝缘态、拓扑等涌现现象。但此前莫尔现象主要在人工组装的低维范德华异质结中研究，扭转角和晶格对准依赖器件制备过程。作者希望探索本征生长的块体莫尔晶体这一互补平台，其中层间晶格失配在体相中形成相干莫尔超晶格，以研究超越二维异质结的关联量子现象。

Method: 对最近报道的块体莫尔材料 (Sr6TaS8)1+x(TaS2)8 单晶，在常压下进行了电输运、磁化率、比热测量（同时包含多晶样品），系统表征其超导转变；并通过输运测量在约 270 K 处发现显著异常，提示存在电荷密度波转变。

Result: 该材料在常压下表现出体超导电性，超导转变温度 Tc = 2.5 K，且电输运、磁化率和比热三种测量在单晶与多晶样品上结果一致；输运测量在近 270 K 处出现明显异常，暗示该莫尔体系中存在电荷密度波转变。

Conclusion: 体超导的观测确立超导是本征合成莫尔材料中的一种涌现相，并表明块体莫尔晶体是研究超越人工组装二维异质结的关联量子现象的有前景平台。

Abstract: Moire materials provide a versatile platform realizing emergent electronic states arising from enhanced correlation due to flat bands. A variety of phenomena including superconductivity, low dimensional ferromagnetism, Mott insulating phase, topological phenomena have been reported in such systems. To date, moire phenomena have been predominantly explored in artificially assembled low-dimensional van der Waals heterostructures, where relative twist and lattice alignment are controlled during device fabrication. Recently, intrinsically grown bulk moire crystals have emerged as a complementary materials platform, in which lattice mismatch between constituent layers generates a coherent moire superlattice throughout the bulk crystal. Here we report the evidence of bulk superconductivity in single crystals of a recently reported bulk Moire materials (Sr6TaS8)1+x(TaS2)8 under ambient pressure conditions. The material exhibits a superconducting transition at Tc = 2.5 K, evidenced consistently by electrical transport, magnetic susceptibility, and heat-capacity measurements on single-crystal and polycrystalline samples. Transport measurements reveal a pronounced anomaly near 270 K, suggestive of a charge-density-wave transition in this moire system. The observation of bulk superconductivity establishes superconductivity as an emergent phase in this intrinsically synthesized moire material and highlights bulk moire crystals as a promising platform for investigating correlated quantum phenomena beyond artificially assembled two-dimensional heterostructures.

</details>


### [111] [Symmetry-selective field-induced triplet superconductivity in Ising-superconductor monolayer NbSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06752)
*Sudipta Biswas,Sudhansu S. Mandal,A. Taraphder*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过自洽BdG方法研究单层NbSe₂（伊辛超导体）在面内塞曼场下等自旋三重态配对的产生，发现母体单线态对称性决定了诱导的三重态对称性，并给出(h_x, μ)平面相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究单层NbSe₂这类伊辛超导体在面内磁场下如何产生等自旋三重态配对，以及三重态配对对称性与母体单线态凝聚体之间的关系，为场可调混合宇称超导态提供理论依据。

Method: 采用自洽Bogoliubov–de Gennes（BdG）方法，使用单层NbSe₂三角晶格的实际跃迁参数，在手征（on-site）、最近邻和次近邻配对通道下，确定不同化学势μ范围内能量上有利的单线态配对态；随后在(h_x, μ)平面绘制相图，并做动量分辨分析（考察布里渊区Γ、K、K'点附近的主导贡献），同时研究Rashba自旋轨道耦合的影响。

Result: 面内磁场有助于诱导三重态配对，其对称性由母体单线态决定；对于非局域配对通道，手征d波配对在能量上有利，并诱导出具有相反手征性的手征p波等自旋三重态分量；Rashba自旋轨道耦合会抑制超导序并降低临界场。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了母体单线态凝聚体对称性与涌现三重态超导之间的直接联系，表明单层NbSe₂是实现场可调混合宇称超导态的有前景平台。

Abstract: We investigate superconductivity in monolayer NbSe$_2$, an Ising superconductor, under an in-plane Zeeman field $h_x$, focusing on the emergence of symmetry selected equal-spin triplet pairing. Using a self-consistent Bogoliubov--de Gennes approach with realistic hopping parameters for monolayer NbSe$_2$ on a triangular lattice, we determine the energetically favored singlet pairing states in a range of chemical potentials $μ$ for onsite, nearest-neighbor, and next-nearest-neighbor pairing channels, and map the resulting phase diagram in $(h_x,μ)$ plane. A momentum-resolved analysis reveals distinct dominant contributions in order parameters arise from the surroundings of $Γ$, $K$, and $K'$ points of the Brillouin zone. We find that an in-plane magnetic field helps to induce a triplet pairing whose symmetry is determined by the parent singlet state, upon opening the triplet interaction channel. For the nonlocal pairing channels, chiral-$d$ pairing is energetically favored and it induces chiral-$p$ equal-spin triplet component with opposite chirality. We further find that Rashba spin-orbit coupling, relevant to substrate coupling and electrostatic gating, suppresses the superconducting orders and reduces the critical field. Our results establish a direct link between the symmetry of the parent singlet condensate and the emergent triplet superconductivity, highlighting monolayer NbSe$_2$ as a promising platform for field-tunable mixed-parity superconducting states.

</details>


### [112] [Nearly Isotropic Vortex Solid in $\mathbf{(La,Pr)_{3}Ni_{2}O_{7}}$ Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07510)
*Yaolong Bian,Yaqi Chen,Heng Wang,Guangdi Zhou,Fei Peng,Zichen Lv,Jiaqiang Cai,Yifan Chen,Wenjie Meng,Ze Wang,Haoliang Huang,Daohua Zhang,Mingliang Tian,Jinfeng Jia,Qi-kun Xue,Zhuoyu Chen,Jinglei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过同步电阻与抗磁测量，在(La,Pr)₃Ni₂O₇薄膜中测量涡旋熔化相图，发现涡旋熔化场的各向异性参数随温度降低趋近于1，表明超流密度近各向同性，为3d_{z²}衍生的γ带对超导有实质贡献提供了热力学证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍酸盐中Ni 3d_{z²}衍生的γ带在超导中的作用尚未解决，需通过涡旋熔化各向异性判断超流密度来源及维度特征。

Method: 对(La,Pr)₃Ni₂O₇薄膜进行同步电阻与抗磁测量，分别在面内和面外磁场下绘制涡旋熔化相图；利用各向异性Ginzburg-Landau标度关系 H_m^{ab}/H_m^c = √(ρ_s^{ab}/ρ_s^c) 关联涡旋熔化场各向异性与超流密度各向异性。

Result: 对H∥c，几何受限效应产生pancake涡旋；涡旋熔化场各向异性参数 γ_{H_m} = H_m^{ab}/H_m^c 随温度降低单调减小并在低温趋近于1，表明近各向同性的超流密度。

Conclusion: 近各向同性的涡旋固态与严格二维的3d_{x²−y²}衍生能带不相容，但可由3d_{z²}衍生的γ带提供的显著层间超流贡献自然解释。该结果为γ带对双层镍酸盐薄膜超导的实质贡献提供了热力学证据。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in bulk bilayer nickelates has established a new platform for exploring high-$T_c$ superconductivity beyond the cuprates. The role of the Ni $3d_{z^2}$-derived $γ$ band in the superconductivity of bilayer nickelates remains unresolved. By performing simultaneous resistance and diamagnetism measurements on (La,Pr)$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ thin films, we map the vortex melting phase diagram for both in-plane and out-of-plane magnetic fields. For $H\parallel c$, the geometric confinement effect gives rise to pancake vortices. Remarkably, the anisotropy parameter of the vortex melting field $γ_{H_m} \equiv H_m^{ab}/H_m^c$ decreases monotonically with decreasing temperature and approaches unity at low temperatures. Within the anisotropic Ginzburg--Landau scaling, $H_m^{ab}/H_m^c = \sqrt{ρ_s^{ab}/ρ_s^c}$ tracks the superfluid-density anisotropy. Such a vortex solid implies a nearly isotropic superfluid density, which is irreconcilable with the strictly two-dimensional $3d_{x^2-y^2}$-derived bands, but naturally explained by a substantial interlayer superfluid contribution from the $3d_{z^2}$-derived $γ$ band. Our results provide thermodynamic evidence for a substantial contribution of the $γ$ band to superconductivity in bilayer nickelate thin films.

</details>


### [113] [Doping-controlled topological superconducting transition in misfit layer compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07545)
*Hugo Le Du,Robin Salvatore,Justine Cordiez,Ludovica Zullo,Arindam Mukherjee,Daniel Schmieg,Dominik Volavka,Francois Debontridder,Marie Herve,Tomas Samuely,Shunsuke Sasaki,Florent Pawula,Etienne Janod,Laurent Cario,Tristan Cren*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在体材料错配层化合物 (LaxPb1-xSe)1.14(NbSe2)2 中，通过掺杂调控实现了从常规超导到拓扑超导的相变，并揭示了由非超导相分隔的两个超导区域，为拓扑超导的工程化提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现拓扑超导是通往容错量子计算的关键路径，核心挑战在于能否连续驱动材料跨越拓扑量子相变，直接观测从平凡超导到拓扑超导的演化；但缺少一个能实现如此极端且精确调谐的稳健平台。

Method: 作者以体错配层化合物 (LaxPb1-xSe)1.14(NbSe2)2 为对象，通过 La 掺杂实现对载流子浓度/化学势的精确调控，测量其非单调相图，并借助对非磁性无序的敏感性、原子台阶边缘的取向选择性带内模式等空间分辨实验表征，辅以 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 计算进行理论验证。

Result: 揭示了一个非单调相图：存在两个由非超导相在特定掺杂点分隔的超导区域。高掺杂区超导相对非磁性无序异常敏感，并在原子台阶边缘出现取向选择性的带内模式；BdG 计算表明这些空间特征与时间反演对称的晶体拓扑序参量一致。

Conclusion: 实验与理论一致表明，(LaxPb1-xSe)1.14(NbSe2)2 中通过掺杂可调控实现时间反演对称的晶体拓扑超导态，确立了错配层化合物作为工程化拓扑超导的可行平台。

Abstract: Achieving topological superconductivity is a key goal in quantum physics, offering a path to fault-tolerant quantum computers. A central challenge in this field is to continuously drive a material through a topological quantum phase transition to directly observe the evolution from trivial to topological superconductivity. However, finding a robust platform that allows such extreme and precise tuning remains a challenge. Here, we demonstrate a doping-controlled phase transition from a conventional to a topological superconducting state in the bulk misfit layer compound (LaxPb1-xSe)1.14(NbSe2)2. We reveal a non-monotonic phase diagram characterized by two distinct superconducting regimes separated by a non-superconducting phase at a precise doping. In the highly doped regime, the superconducting phase becomes remarkably sensitive to non-magnetic disorder, and orientation-selective in-gap modes emerge at atomic step edges. Supported by Bogoliubov-de Gennes calculations, these emergent spatial signatures are consistent with a time-reversal-symmetric crystalline-topological order parameter. Our results establish misfit compounds as a platform to engineering topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [114] [Interference-controlled magnetic bound states and subgap transport in a Square--octagon superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07546)
*Ravi Kiran*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了 s 波超导方-八边晶格上磁性吸附原子的行为，发现平带的大谱权重并不必然导致强杂质-宿主耦合：方空心位吸附因破坏性干涉强烈抑制与平带的杂化，而顶位与八边空心位吸附则保留显著杂化，这种选择性塑造了磁束缚态并体现在非局域亚能隙电导中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方-八边晶格因多子晶格结构，使得不同吸附几何可探测宿主电子态的不同组合，但平带态的大谱权重与杂质-宿主耦合强度之间的关系并不显然，需要厘清吸附几何、窄带/平带态与亚能隙输运间的相互作用。

Method: 在 s 波超导方-八边晶格中设置磁性吸附原子，对比方空心、顶位与八边空心三种吸附几何，分析其与平带流形的波函数重叠与杂化，计算磁束缚态，并在正常-超导-正常结几何下求解非局域亚能隙电导及其对交换劈裂的依赖。

Result: 方空心吸附因破坏性干涉强烈抑制与平带流形的重叠，主要支持近原子态；顶位与八边空心吸附则与超导宿主强杂化。该效应随相关能带变平而连续增强，并在材料启发的参数区间保持显著。在正常-超导-正常结构中，代表性交换劈裂下方空心吸附压低低偏压电导、八边空心吸附增强电导，且方空心响应对交换劈裂的依赖更尖锐。

Conclusion: 窄带和平带超导体中磁性吸附原子的响应由可用的谱权重以及杂质-宿主杂化的对称性与波函数结构共同决定，而非仅由平带谱权重大小决定。

Abstract: The square--octagon lattice provides a useful setting for studying magnetic adatoms because its multisublattice structure allows different adsorption geometries to probe distinct combinations of the host electronic states. We study magnetic adatoms in an $s$-wave superconducting square--octagon lattice, focusing on the interplay between adsorption geometry, narrow and flat-band states, and subgap transport. We find that the large spectral weight associated with a flat band does not necessarily imply strong impurity--host coupling. For symmetric square-hollow adsorption, destructive interference strongly suppresses the overlap with the flat-band manifold, whereas top and octagon-hollow adsorption retain substantial overlap. This selectivity is reflected in the magnetic bound states, with the square-hollow channel supporting a predominantly atomic state, while the top and octagon-hollow states are strongly hybridized with the superconducting host. The effect develops continuously as the relevant band becomes flatter and remains pronounced in a material-motivated parameter regime. In a normal--superconductor--normal geometry, these differences are also reflected in the nonlocal subgap conductance. At a representative exchange splitting, square-hollow adsorption suppresses the low-bias conductance relative to the clean device, whereas octagon-hollow adsorption enhances it, with the square-hollow response showing a much sharper dependence on exchange splitting. Our results show that the magnetic-adatom response in narrow and flat-band superconductors is governed by the available spectral weight and by the symmetry and wave-function structure of the impurity--host hybridization.

</details>


### [115] [Signatures of nodal superconductivity in stoichiometric FeTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08116)
*Cequn Li,Zi-Jie Yan,Yang Ge,Zihao Wang,Bing Xia,Stephen Paolini,Pu Xiao,Lok-Kan Lai,Jiatao Song,Austin R. Kaczmarek,Lujin Min,Kenji Yasuda,Peter J. Hirschfeld,Jiabin Yu,Cui-Zu Chang,Katja C. Nowack*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过多种实验手段和平均场计算，研究了化学计量比 FeTe 薄膜的超导配对态，发现其具有微米尺度的空间不均匀性、不发散（幂律）的伦敦穿透深度、V 形低能态密度等特征，表明其配对态存在节点或深能隙极小值，符合 d 波或 nodal s 波配对，确立了 FeTe 作为铁硫族超导体相图中一个独特的超导区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学计量比 FeTe 的超导性使研究者得以触及 Fe(Se,Te) 相图的 FeTe 端，但其超导配对态的性质一直未解决。作者旨在阐明 FeTe 薄膜的局域超流响应与配对对称性，以补充铁硫族化合物超导配对态演化的图像。

Method: 作者综合运用扫描超导量子干涉器件（SQUID）显微镜、电输运测量、扫描隧道显微镜与谱学（STM/S）以及平均场计算，对具有可调化学计量比的 FeTe 薄膜进行局域超流密度、超流刚度、伦敦穿透深度和低能态密度的表征与两能隙建模分析。

Result: 即使在化学计量比 FeTe 中，仍观察到超流刚度和超导转变温度 Tc 的微米尺度空间变化；伦敦穿透深度低至 0.02Tc 仍不饱和，并呈幂律温度依赖（指数约 1–1.5）。结合 V 形低能态密度和两能隙建模，结果表明存在能隙节点或深极小值，与 d 波或 nodal s 波超导态一致。此外，随超流刚度降低，Tc 表现出由弱抑制到快速抑制的转变。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了化学计量比 FeTe 为一种独特的超导区，偏离了中等 Se/Te 成分下能隙趋于各向同性的趋势，为铁硫族超导的现代微观理论提供了新基准；同时揭示的 Tc 与超流刚度关系将 FeTe 与非常规超导体的更广泛现象学联系起来。

Abstract: Superconductivity in stoichiometric FeTe opens access to the FeTe endpoint of the Fe(Se,Te) phase diagram, yet the nature of its superconducting pairing state remains unresolved. In this work, we combine scanning superconducting quantum interference device (SQUID) microscopy, electrical transport, scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy (STM/S), and mean-field calculations to investigate the local superfluid response and pairing state of FeTe thin films with tunable stoichiometry. Even in stoichiometric FeTe, we observe micrometer-scale spatial variations in both superfluid stiffness and superconducting transition temperature $T_c$, while the London penetration depth remains non-saturating down to 0.02$T_c$ and follows a power-law temperature dependence with an exponent of approximately 1-1.5. Together with a V-shaped low-energy density of states and two-gap modeling, these results indicate a superconducting state with gap nodes or deep minima, consistent with either a $d$-wave or nodal $s$-wave superconducting state. Our findings establish stoichiometric FeTe as a distinct superconducting regime that departs from the trend toward more isotropic gaps at intermediate Se/Te compositions, providing a new benchmark for modern microscopic theories of iron-chalcogenide superconductivity. Our work also reveals a crossover from weak to rapid suppression of $T_c$ as superfluid stiffness decreases, connecting FeTe to the broader phenomenology observed in unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [116] [Magnetochiral anisotropy of thermally activated phase slips in noncentrosymmetric superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08237)
*Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文发展了缺乏反演对称性（含Rashba自旋轨道耦合、面内磁场）的扩散型窄超导导线中热激活相位滑移的理论，预言相滑移电阻具有磁手性各向异性，其非互易性源自对电流和磁场均奇异的激活势垒不对称性，且与同一导线的超导二极管效率通过普适数值比固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导窄导线临界温度以下的电阻拖尾由相位滑移主导，但缺乏反演对称性体系中热激活相位滑移的非互易输运行为尚未被系统理解，特别是其与超导二极管效应的定量联系。

Method: 将导线微观建模为由二维电子气形成的扩散型导线，引入Rashba自旋轨道耦合与面内磁场；基于自由能的Lifshitz不变量（三次梯度项、动量奇异的四次顶角，以及由螺旋基态提升为奇异的偶梯度项），推导热激活相位滑移的激活势垒不对称性，并将其与超导二极管效率建立普适数值比；动力学Lifshitz不变量对涨落前因子仅作参数次主导的修正。

Result: 相滑移电阻获得磁手性各向异性：固定电流时随磁场反向而改变，固定磁场时随电流反向而改变；线性响应电阻在Onsager互易性要求下保持磁场对称。势垒不对称性由激活指数承载，磁手性信号相对二极管不对称性被放大（势垒高度与温度之比），表现为微分电阻指数斜率随磁场反对称的劈裂。

Conclusion: 热激活相位滑移的非互易性由与超导二极管效应相同的自由能项组合决定，二者通过无自由参数的普适数值比联系，且磁手性信号因处于激活指数而得到放大，可通过微分电阻迹线的反对称劈裂实验探测。

Abstract: Resistive tails of narrow superconducting wires below the critical temperature are governed by phase slips, thermally activated near the transition and quantum at lower temperatures. We develop the theory of thermally activated phase slips in wires lacking inversion symmetry, modeled microscopically as a diffusive wire formed from a two-dimensional electron gas with Rashba spin-orbit coupling in an in-plane magnetic field. The phase-slip resistance acquires magnetochiral anisotropy: at fixed current it changes upon reversal of the field, and at fixed field upon reversal of the current, while the linear-response resistance remains field symmetric as required by Onsager reciprocity. The nonreciprocity originates from an asymmetry of the phase-slip activation barrier that is odd in both current and field. It is generated by the Lifshitz invariants of the free energy, the cubic gradient term and the momentum-odd quartic vertex, together with even gradient terms promoted to odd ones by the helical ground state, precisely the combination that determines the superconducting diode effect. The barrier asymmetry is therefore fixed by the diode efficiency of the same wire through a universal numerical ratio, with no adjustable parameters. The kinetic Lifshitz invariant, which makes relaxation and noise of the order parameter nonreciprocal, affects only the fluctuation prefactor at a parametrically subleading level. Because the asymmetry resides in the activation exponent, the magnetochiral signal is amplified relative to the diode asymmetry by the ratio of barrier height to temperature, and appears as a field-antisymmetric splitting of the exponential slopes of differential-resistance traces routinely used to identify thermally activated phase slips.

</details>


### [117] [Néel-Vector Control of the Josephson Diode Effect in $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08278)
*Xian-Tang Xu,Xun-Jiang Luo,Mingliang Tian,Ning Hao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究由常规s波超导体与$\PT$对称共线反铁磁体（以CuMnAs为模型）构成的约瑟夫森结，发现其同时呈现约瑟夫森二极管效应和$\varphi_0$结态，二者均由Néel矢量调控：旋转90°可关闭两种效应，反转则切换二极管极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导与磁性相互作用在约瑟夫森结中产生丰富现象，而$\PT$对称反铁磁体的特殊对称性可能为实现无外场、高可调的约瑟夫森二极管和$\varphi_0$结提供新平台。

Method: 采用微观建模与对称性分析，并发展通道分辨散射理论以刻画反常相位；利用格林函数约化识别出单一重整化$\PT$简并能带作为输运载流子。

Result: 结同时表现出约瑟夫森二极管效应和$\varphi_0$结态；Néel矢量旋转90°关闭两种效应，反转Néel矢量则切换二极管极性。通道分辨散射理论精确刻画反常相位并给出二极管效应的严格条件，通道电流-相位关系叠加产生可观且可由交换场大小和方向调控的二极管效率。格林函数约化精确复现整体电流幅度。

Conclusion: $\PT$对称反铁磁体是实现无外场、高度可调约瑟夫森二极管和$\varphi_0$结的多功能平台。

Abstract: The interplay of superconductivity and magnetism gives rise to rich phenomena in Josephson junctions. In this Letter, we study Josephson junctions formed by conventional $s$-wave superconductors and a $\PT$-symmetric collinear antiferromagnet modeled on CuMnAs. Using microscopic modeling and symmetry analysis, we show that these junctions exhibit both the Josephson diode effect and $\varphi_{0}$-junction states. Remarkably, both effects are controlled by the Néel vector: rotating it by $90^{\circ}$ switches off both, while reversing it switches the diode polarity. To reveal the microscopic mechanism, we develop a channel-resolved scattering theory that accurately captures the anomalous phases and establishes the exact condition for the diode effect. The interplay of the channel current-phase relations yields a sizable diode efficiency, tunable by both the magnitude and direction of the exchange field. Furthermore, a Green-function reduction identifies a single renormalized $\PT$-degenerate band as the transport carrier and precisely reproduces the full current amplitudes. Our work establishes $\PT$-symmetric antiferromagnets as versatile platforms for field-free, highly tunable Josephson diodes and $\varphi_{0}$ junctions.

</details>


### [118] [$1/3$-Flux Bound States in Multicomponent Superconductor Exhibiting Non-Abelian Statistics of $\mathbb {Z}_3$ Parafermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08326)
*Xing-Yu Wu,Y. H. Wang,X. C. Xie,Yijia Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出多组分超导体中1/3磁通束缚态展现出Z3仿费米子的非阿贝尔统计，并给出了通过费米子占据数读出和交叉安德烈耶夫反射实现完备编织操作的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分超导体预言可承载任意分数超导磁通量子，近期进展使人们期待在具有C3旋转对称性的体系中涌现1/3磁通量子。束缚于这种分数磁通的电子长期以来被认为是任意子，但其明确的编织统计一直未被探索。

Method: 作者提出1/3磁通束缚态(1/3-FBSs)具有Z3仿费米子的非阿贝尔统计。在禁止双占据约束下，两次连续的1/3-FBS编织操作等价于一次Z3仿费米子编织操作；编码编织结果的仿费米子宇称可通过1/3-FBS的费米子占据数读出；结合交叉安德烈耶夫反射诱导的Hadamard门，可实现完整的Z3仿费米子编织操作集合。

Result: 证明1/3-FBS的两次编织操作等价于一次Z3仿费米子编织，其仿费米子宇称可经费米子占据数读出，并可通过交叉安德烈耶夫反射诱导的Hadamard门组合出完整的Z3仿费米子编织操作。

Conclusion: 1/3磁通束缚态展现出Z3仿费米子的非阿贝尔统计，为在多组分超导体中实现和操控分数通量任意子、探索拓扑量子计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Multicomponent superconductors (MSCs) are predicted to host magnetic flux quanta carrying arbitrary fractions of the superconducting flux quantum. Recent advances have raised the expectation that $1/3$ flux quanta may emerge in MSCs with $C_3$ rotational symmetry. Electrons bound to such fractional flux are long regarded to form anyons, yet their explicit braiding statistics remain unexplored. We propose that these 1/3-flux bound states (1/3-FBSs) exhibit the intriguing non-Abelian statistics of $\mathbb{Z}_3$ parafermions. Under no-double-occupancy constraint, two successive braiding operations of 1/3-FBSs are equivalent to a single $\mathbb Z_3$ parafermion braiding operation. The parafermion parity encoding the braiding outcome can be read out via the fermionic occupation number of the 1/3-FBSs. Combined with a crossed-Andreev-reflection-induced Hadamard gate, one can realize the complete set of $\mathbb{Z}_3$ parafermion braiding operations.

</details>


### [119] [Geometry-induced current decrowding in superconducting 3D constrictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08349)
*Igor Bogush,Oleksandr Dobrovolskiy*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文论证超导纳米结构的3D几何塑形可通过电流重新分布克服几何电流拥挤效应，实现对临界电流的调控和新型涡旋动力学（包括非互易临界电流与涡旋线的3D分裂）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电流拥挤是纳米尺度器件的普遍限制，在超导体中它会抑制序参量、促进涡旋成核，使临界电流低于内在的拆对极限。作者希望探索能否通过3D几何设计改变电流分布，从而突破这一几何限制并开拓新的功能。

Method: 基于含时Ginzburg–Landau方程的有限元建模，对3D超导纳米结构（如3D收缩结构）进行数值模拟，研究其在面内中等磁场下的响应、电流再分布以及涡旋成核与演化过程。

Result: 3D收缩结构在中等面内磁场下表现出显著且可调的响应，而平面几何不具备该功能；实现了几何诱导的“去拥挤”效应、临界电流调制，并发现非互易临界电流等新型涡旋动力学。此外，曲率与有限厚度改变了涡旋成核机制：涡旋线经顶点进入后会发生3D分裂成两条涡旋细丝，这一机理在2D流形中不存在。

Conclusion: 3D几何工程可作为超导纳米结构的设计范式，在接近拆对极限的器件中实现对电流分布与涡旋动力学的控制。

Abstract: Current crowding is a ubiquitous limitation in nanoscale devices, where confinement and sharp features distort current flow, generating localized current-density hotspots and premature failure. In superconductors, this effect suppresses the order parameter and promotes vortex nucleation, reducing the critical current below the intrinsic depairing limit. Here, we demonstrate that 3D shaping of superconducting nanoarchitectures overcomes geometric current crowding through spatial current redistribution, establishing a geometry-induced decrowding effect. Using finite-element modeling based on the time-dependent Ginzburg--Landau equation, we reveal that 3D constrictions exhibit a pronounced and tunable response to moderate in-plane magnetic fields---a functionality absent in planar geometries. This field-controlled geometry enables critical-current modulation and unveils new vortex-dynamics regimes, including a non-reciprocal critical current. Furthermore, curvature and finite thickness fundamentally alter vortex nucleation by enabling apex-mediated entry of single vortex lines, followed by their 3D splitting into two vortex filaments---a mechanism that does not occur in 2D manifolds. Our findings demonstrate 3D geometric engineering as a design paradigm for superconducting nanoarchitectures, offering control over current distribution and vortex dynamics in devices approaching the depairing limit.

</details>


### [120] [Thomas-Fermi screening of electrostatic fields in a type-I superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08516)
*Nikhil Seeja Sivakumar,Tyler James,Frederick Carlisle,Philip Moriarty,Brian Kiraly*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过qPlus原子力显微镜与扫描隧道显微镜/谱学联用技术，在约340 mK低温下对单晶Pb(111)表面测量纵向静电屏蔽，发现超导转变前后屏蔽变化小于约1%，远小于Hirsch理论预测，支持Thomas-Fermi屏蔽在超导态下保持不变的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验伦敦方程对超导体纵向与横向电动力学响应给出不同预言：传统框架将迈斯纳效应归于横向分量，并认为纵向响应在进入超导态后不变，静电场仍按Thomas-Fermi长度（约1 Å）屏蔽。但Hirsch提出的替代理论预测，当系统冷却至超导转变温度Tc以下时，纵向屏蔽长度应由伦敦穿透深度决定。本文旨在通过实验检验这两种相互矛盾的预测。

Method: 在约340 mK（T/Tc约0.05）下，对单晶Pb(111)样品结合qPlus原子力显微镜与扫描隧道显微镜/谱学测量。通过施加200 mT垂直表面的磁场淬灭超导电性，在同一采样位置分别在有磁和无磁条件下测量力-距离曲线和局域场发射共振谱，以探测纵向针尖电场的静电屏蔽变化。

Result: 测量将正常态与超导态之间屏蔽的变化约束在小于约1%，比Hirsch理论预测值小两到三个数量级，与超导转变前后Thomas-Fermi屏蔽保持不变完全一致。

Conclusion: 实验不支持Hirsch关于纵向屏蔽长度由伦敦穿透深度决定的理论，证实超导转变过程不改变Thomas-Fermi静电屏蔽，纵向响应与经验伦敦方程的传统解释一致。

Abstract: The empirical London equations make distinct predictions for a superconductor's longitudinal and transverse electrodynamic response. Conventionally, this framework attributes the Meissner effect to the transverse component, with the longitudinal response predicted to remain unchanged on entering the superconducting state, i.e. a static electric field is screened over the Thomas-Fermi length ($λ_{\mathrm{TF}} \sim 1$~Å), just as in the nonsupercondcuting metal. J.~E.~Hirsch~[\textit{Phys.~ Rev.~B}~\textbf{69},~214515~(2004)] has developed an alternative formalism, which predicts that the longitudinal screening length should instead be governed by the London penetration depth as the system is cooled below the superconducting transition temperature ($T_{\mathrm{c}}$). Here we use a combination of qPlus atomic force microscopy and scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy of single-crystal Pb(111) at $\sim$~340~mK~ \mbox{($T/T_{\mathrm{c}}\sim0.05$)} in an attempt to detect any modifications in electrostatic screening of the longitudinal tip field upon entering the superconducting phase. We quench superconductivity using a magnetic field of 200 mT normal to the Pb(111) surface. By measuring force-distance curves and local field-emission resonance spectra at the same sample position in the presence and absence of the magnetic field, we constrain the change in screening between the normal and superconducting state to less than $\sim 1 \%$. This is between two to three orders of magnitude smaller than that predicted by Hirsch's theory, and entirely consistent with unmodified Thomas-Fermi screening across the superconducting transition.

</details>


### [121] [Fabrication And Characterization Of High-Quality Nb/Al-AlO$_x$/Nb Cross-Type Josephson Tunnel Junctions Utilising CMP-based Planarisation Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08569)
*Alexander Stoll,Andreas Reifenberger,Daniel Hengstler,Andreas Fleischmann,Christian Enss*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用化学机械抛光（CMP）替代 lift-off 工艺，在晶圆尺度上制备嵌入溅射SiO₂中的 Nb/Al-AlOx/Nb 十字型约瑟夫森结，成品率超过90%，并表征了不同面积结的电学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有窗口型约瑟夫森结虽可靠可重复，但存在光刻对准误差和不可避免的寄生电容，限制 DC SQUID 的能量灵敏度；而 lift-off 工艺耗时且可能在微结构边缘留下影响后续层的"翼状"残留。因此需要一种能实现更小结区、消除寄生电容且高效可靠的晶圆级制备工艺。

Method: 采用十字型（cross-type）几何结构制备 Nb/Al-AlOx/Nb 约瑟夫森结，将三层膜嵌入溅射沉积的 SiO₂ 中以实现结构平坦化和底电极侧壁可靠绝缘；使用化学机械抛光（CMP）替代 lift-off 去除多余 SiO₂，获得平滑均匀的表面。随后通过 IV 特性和 Fraunhofer 图样提取结的品质参数，并对不同结面积（最小至 1μm×1μm）在晶圆上的分布进行表征。

Result: CMP 工艺比 lift-off 更快且产生平滑均匀的表面，有效提高了结制备的可靠性与重复性，晶圆级成品率超过90%。论文还讨论了制备细节对结质量及电学性质的影响，并提取了不同面积结的品质参数。

Conclusion: 采用 CMP 配合嵌入 SiO₂ 的十字型约瑟夫森结制备方案，可克服窗口型结的对准误差与寄生电容问题，并取代耗时且易留残留的 lift-off 工艺，实现高成品率、高可靠性的晶圆级超导器件制备。

Abstract: Josephson tunnel junctions (JJs) are the fundamental building blocks of today's most advanced superconducting electronic components, such as qubits and superconducting quantum interference devices (SQUIDs). Given the ongoing demand for scalability of these devices on the wafer-scale, it is crucial to control the fabrication process as precisely as possible to ensure uniform quality and reproducibility. Today, window-type JJs are often used in the production of DC SQUIDs and while they are reliable and reproducible, they suffer from alignment inaccuracies caused by photolithography and unavoidable parasitic capacitances, thus limiting the energy sensitivity of DC SQUIDs. These problems can be circumvented by producing JJs with cross-type geometry, which allows for smaller junction areas and eliminates parasitic capacitances. Here we discuss the fabrication of Nb/Al-AlOx/Nb-based cross-type JJs, where the trilayer is embedded in sputter-deposited SiO$_2$ to allow for planarisation of the structures and to ensure the reliable insulation of the sidewalls of the bottom electrode. Instead of lift-off processes that require a lot of time and potentially leave unwanted wings along the edges of microstructures behind that might compromise subsequent layers, we use chemical-mechanical polishing (CMP) for the removal of excess SiO$_2$. This is not only much faster, but produces smooth and uniform surfaces, which in turn effectively improves the reliability of our JJ production process resulting in a high yield of over $90\,\%$ on wafer-scale. In addition, we discuss the influence of fabrication details on the quality and electrical properties of our JJs with different junction areas down to $1\,\mathrm{μm} \times 1\,\mathrm{μm}$ distributed across the wafer and extract the junction specific quality parameters from their IV-characteristics and their Fraunhofer patterns.

</details>


### [122] [Junction-free polarity-tunable superconducting diode effect enables XNOR logic operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09097)
*Alapan Bera,Soumik Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了在NbSe2/F3GT异质结构中实现的无结型、全范德华超导二极管效应，借助铁磁近邻诱导的对称性破缺，获得了高达30%的二极管效率，并演示了XNOR逻辑门功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高效超导二极管依赖约瑟夫森结弱连接结构，存在制备复杂、电噪声大、依赖结质量等局限。作者希望实现无需弱连接结的高效超导二极管，以降低制造复杂度与噪声，推动无耗散逻辑电路发展。

Method: 构建全范德华异质结构，将超导体NbSe2与强单轴铁磁体Fe3GeTe2（F3GT）耦合，利用铁磁近邻效应打破NbSe2的反演/时间反演对称性，从而实现非互易超流。通过外加磁场与F3GT自旋极化方向调控二极管极性。

Result: 在仅50 mT的小磁场下实现高达30%的超导二极管效率；实现无外场（field-free）的二极管整流效应，且极性可由F3GT自旋极化方向调控；利用SDE对有效场的依赖，演示了双输入XNOR逻辑门操作。

Conclusion: 研究证明磁近邻效应可用于实现无结型超导二极管，为高性能、高能效逻辑电路提供了新途径，并凸显了铁磁/超导范德华异质结构在非互易超流器件中的应用潜力。

Abstract: The realization of the superconducting diode effect (SDE), characterized by non-reciprocal supercurrent, is a major step towards dissipationless logic circuits. A polarity-tunable superconducting diode with high diode efficiency primarily consists of Josephson-junction architectures that couple two superconducting regions via a weak link. Realizing an SDE without a weak-link junction would reduce fabrication complexity, lower electrical noise, and minimize dependence on junction quality. Here, we report an all-vdW, highly efficient, junction-free SDE in NbSe2, enabled by proximity-induced symmetry breaking from a strong uniaxial ferromagnet Fe3GeTe2 (F3GT). Superconducting diode efficiency of up to 30% is achieved at a small magnetic field of 50 mT. A field-free diode rectification effect is realized with diode polarity that is tunable by the F3GT spin polarization. Furthermore, by employing the active-field dependence of the SDE, a two-input exclusive-NOR (XNOR) logic gate operation is demonstrated. The findings highlight the potential utilization of magnetic proximity effect towards realizing junction-free superconducting diodes for high-performance, energy-efficient logic circuits.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Bridging simulation length scales with cellular automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05696)
*John J. Karnes,Esteban D. Gadea,Shakkira Erimban,Ignacio J. Bombau,Valeria Molinero*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于元胞自动机（CA）的方法，从高保真模拟配置中学习局部规则，用于生成任意大尺寸的粗粒化晶格起始构型，解决多尺度模拟中粗粒化模型无法自行组装、必须继承细粒度构型的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度模拟中，粗粒化模型自身无法从任意堆积自组装成物理上有意义的构型，必须继承更高保真度模拟的起始构型。因此粗粒化模型可达的长度尺度受限于能够经济地平衡的最大细粒度构型，需一种能生成更大起始构型的方法。

Method: 采用元胞自动机（CA）方法，以高保真模拟所得构型为训练集，通过逻辑回归学习短程局部 CA 规则；用短期规则反复演化晶格以产生长程有序结构。以水合阴离子交换膜（AEM）为原理验证对象，学习规则并生成新起始构型，再在介观 kMC 模拟中与 MD 导出的"真实基准"对比。

Result: 为水合 AEM 开发了 CA 规则并生成新起始构型，其在介观 kMC 模拟中的表现与 MD 导出的基准一致；进一步证明了能够生成大幅更大的晶格，且其 kMC 行为与较小 CA 基准构型一致。

Conclusion: CA 方法能通过从高保真构型中学习规则，高效生成任意大的晶格起始构型，为突破粗粒化模拟长度尺度瓶颈提供了可行方案，并验证了生成构型在介观模拟中的一致性。

Abstract: Multiscale simulation requires coupling physics models that operate at different characteristic length and time scales, because no single method spans the range needed for most problems of interest. Moving to a coarser-grained representation unlocks longer length and time scales, but it discards the microscopic interactions that build morphology. A fine-grained model can be initialized from an arbitrary packing and left to self-assemble into a physically meaningful structure; a lower-resolution model cannot, and must inherit its starting configuration from a higher-fidelity simulation. The length scales accessible to the coarse-grained model are therefore set not by the coarse-grained method itself, but by the largest fine-grained configuration that can be affordably equilibrated. A representative example is the scale-up from particle-based molecular dynamics (MD) to a lattice-based representation such as kinetic Monte Carlo (kMC). In this work, we present a cellular automata (CA) approach for generating arbitrarily large lattice starting configurations. CA is a natural fit for this task: short-ranged local rules drive the evolution of a lattice, and their repeated application gives rise to emergent long-range order, thematically mirroring how short-ranged interactions in MD produce self-assembled morphology. We use a configuration from a higher-fidelity simulation as a training set and learn the CA rules from it via logistic regression. As a proof of principle, we develop these rules for a hydrated anion exchange membrane (AEM), generate new starting configurations, and benchmark their performance in mesoscale kMC simulations against an MD-derived "ground truth." We then demonstrate the ability to generate substantially larger lattices and show that their behavior in kMC is consistent with that of the smaller CA benchmark configurations.

</details>


### [124] [FPBench: Application-Oriented Error Decomposition for Foundation Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05714)
*Kiyan Amirian,Ramanuja Srinivasan Saravanan,Felix Adams,Charles E Schwarz,Yifei Mo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: FPBench 是一个面向应用的基准测试，用于评估基础势（FP）在真实计算任务中的表现，发现平均误差无法预测实际可靠性，并通过误差分解指标定位具体失效来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础势（FPs）在平均能量与力误差上常表现出接近 DFT 的精度，但在实际计算研究中性能不稳定。现有评估方式（平均误差）无法反映其在具体任务中的真实可靠性，因此需要面向应用的基准来弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建 FPBench 应用导向基准，在三个基础任务上评估多个先进 FP：原子模拟的力预测、置换与空位有序的能量排序、离子/空位迁移。引入面向应用的误差分解指标，包括高精度与大误差原子占比、远离平衡原子、相对相稳定性与凸包一致性、离子迁移沿路径误差等。

Result: 平均力与能量误差常无法预测各任务的实际表现，不同 FP 之间的实际可靠性差异显著；误差分解指标能够定位各项计算任务中 FP 误差的具体来源。

Conclusion: FPBench 通过应用导向的任务评估与误差分解指标，为 FP 的严格评估与开发提供了针对性指导，并公开基准、评估代码与排行榜。

Abstract: Foundation potentials (FPs) have emerged as a new basis for atomistic modeling. While their evaluation using average energy and force errors often indicates near-DFT accuracy, their performance in practical computational studies remains inconsistent. Here, we present FPBench, an application-oriented benchmark that evaluates FPs on representative computational tasks. Multiple state-of-the-art FPs are assessed on three fundamental tasks: force prediction for atomistic simulations, energy ranking for substitutional and vacancy orderings, and ion/vacancy migration. Benchmarking these FPs shows that average force and energy errors often fail to predict task performance, revealing substantial differences in practical reliability. FPBench introduces application-oriented error decomposition through metrics that resolve performance according to the physically consequential quantities and configurations governing computational tasks, including the fractions of highly accurate and large-force-error atoms, far-from-equilibrium atoms, relative phase-stability and convex-hull agreement, and along-path errors in ion migration. These error-decomposition metrics identify where FP errors arise within specific computational tasks, providing targeted guidance for model development. FPBench provides an open benchmark, evaluation code, and a public leaderboard for rigorous FP assessment and development.

</details>


### [125] [Plasmon drag and photoinduced magnetic effects in plasmonic and magnetic metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05717)
*Md Afzalur Rab,Terence Baker,Natalia Noginova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究坡莫合金薄膜中光致电压的动力学，发现信号主要由激发的热电子而非热效应机制（如异常能斯特效应）主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲激光照射下等离子体和磁性材料中的光致电效应比电磁动量传递机制的预测高几个数量级，为揭示该效应的本质，需要深入研究不同光激发构型下坡莫合金薄膜中光电电压的动力学特性。

Method: 在不同光电激发构型下，研究坡莫合金薄膜中光电压的动力学过程，将光致电信号分解为磁依赖和磁无关分量，并与异常能斯特效应（ANE）的理论预测贡献进行比较。

Result: 光致电信号包含磁依赖和磁无关两种显著的分量，二者主要跟随激光脉冲的时间轮廓；而异常能斯特效应的预测贡献则远弱且动力学明显更慢。

Conclusion: 这些观察表明，超快光激发产生的热电子（而非热机制）在产生所观测到的光电电压中起主导作用。

Abstract: Photoinduced electric effects in plasmonic and magnetic materials under pulsed laser illumination exceed the prediction of the electromagnetic momentum-transfer mechanism by orders of magnitude. In order to get more information on the nature of the effect we study the kinetics of photovoltages in permalloy thin films at different photoexcitation configurations. The photoinduced electric signals consist of magnetically dependent and magnetically independent components which are of significant magnitude and mainly follow the temporal profile of the laser pulse. In contrast, the predicted contribution from the Anomalous Nernst Effect is much weaker and exhibits substantially slower kinetics. These observations suggest that ultrafast photoexcited hot electrons, rather than thermal mechanisms, play the dominant role in generating the observed photovoltage.

</details>


### [126] [Diffusion Quantum Monte Carlo Benchmark of Interlayer Binding and Charge Redistribution in Chemically Distinct Two-Dimensional Van Der Waals Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05845)
*Kayahan Saritas,Hyeonedok Shin,Jaron T. Krogel,Anouar Benali,P. Ganesh,Paul R. C. Kent*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）为多种双层二维材料建立了系统可控的多体基准，涵盖单层材料、过渡金属二硫化物和磁性过渡金属卤化物，解析层间平衡间距、结合能、层间振动模式与电荷重排，发现常用泛函存在系统性且依赖性质的失效，并揭示了磁性Cr三卤化物中长程偶极尾巴等超越半局域DFT的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中的层间相互作用能产生单层所不具备的涌现现象（非常规磁序、多铁性、拓扑磁相等），但精确预测这些行为需同时处理长程色散、短程轨道杂化和非局域多体电子关联，这对现有高级密度泛函近似仍极具挑战，因此需要一个系统可控的多体基准。

Method: 采用扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）方法，构建一个系统可控的多体基准，覆盖单层材料、过渡金属二硫化物和磁性过渡金属卤化物等多类双层体系；计算平衡层间距、结合能、层间振动模式及电荷重排，并与实验及各泛函（半局域、meta-GGA、色散修正泛函）对比；同时提供可扩展的高性能计算工作流。

Result: DMC结果与现有实验高度吻合；揭示了常用半局域、meta-GGA和色散修正密度泛函存在系统性且依赖于所研究性质的失效；同时DMC解析出细微的层间电荷重排，尤其在磁性Cr三卤化物中产生远超半局域DFT预测范围的长程偶极尾巴，为理解层间耦合的铁性与磁现象提供了多体基础；所获得能量、响应性质和高精度电子密度构成可迁移的基准。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了以DMC为核心的系统多体基准，可定量校正各类密度泛函在不同性质上的误差，并为理解层间耦合的涌现现象提供多体依据；所提供的高性能计算工作流支持科研社区系统性扩展层状量子材料的多体基准数据集，推动下一代密度泛函的开发。

Abstract: Interlayer interactions in two-dimensional materials can generate emergent phenomena absent in their constituent monolayers, including unconventional magnetic order, multiferroicity, and topological magnetic phases. Predicting such emergent behavior requires simultaneously resolving long-range dispersion, short-range orbital hybridization, and electronic correlation interactions that are intrinsically nonlocal and many-body and remain challenging even for advanced density-functional approximations. Here we establish a systematically controlled many-body benchmark for diverse bilayer materials using diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC), spanning single-sheet materials, transition-metal dichalcogenides, and magnetic transition-metal halides. We obtain equilibrium separations, binding energetics, interlayer vibrational modes, and charge redistribution, finding excellent agreement with available experiments while revealing systematic and property-dependent failures across widely used semilocal, meta-GGA, and dispersion-corrected density functionals. Beyond energetics, DMC resolves subtle interlayer charge rearrangements, particularly leading to long-ranged dipolar tails in magnetic Cr trihalides that survive well beyond the regime where semilocal DFT predicts appreciable interlayer polarization, providing a many-body basis for understanding interlayer-coupled ferroic and magnetic phenomena. The resulting energies, response properties, and high-accuracy electron densities constitute a transferable benchmark for developing next-generation density functionals. Finally, we provide a scalable high-performance-computing workflow that enables systematic expansion of many-body benchmark datasets across emerging families of layered quantum materials by the scientific community.

</details>


### [127] [The electromagnetic stress tensor in cubic crystals and amorphous solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05969)
*Richard Dengler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文推导了由点状经典原子构成的三维晶格系统中的电磁真空应力张量，并通过电场的直接推导与介电张量导数的唯象公式两种方式得到一致结果；进一步将随机取向晶粒组成的多晶系统作为非晶固体的模型，通过取向平均得到应力张量，其横向分量与Peierls对液体的结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁真空应力张量通常依赖介电张量的导数这一未知量，作者希望从微观电场的直接计算出发严格推导该量，并考察其在非晶固体中的表现，以弥补宏观电动力学在该问题上的不足。

Method: 对由经典点状原子构成的立方晶格，直接从电场出发计算电磁真空应力张量，并与基于介电张量导数的唯象公式所得结果进行对照验证；随后构建随机取向立方晶粒镶嵌体作为非晶固体模型，通过对立方晶体应力张量在所有取向上的平均得到非晶固体的应力张量。

Result: 两种推导方法得到一致结果；应力张量含有一个非平凡的常数项，因而无法仅由宏观电动力学获得。在非晶固体模型中，取向后仅剩沿电场方向与垂直方向两个分量，其中横向分量与Peierls以完全不同方式得到的液体对应量一致。

Conclusion: 电磁真空应力张量需从微观层面确定，宏观电动力学不足以完整给出该量；通过取向平均，非晶固体的真空应力张量横向分量与液体情形吻合，说明该方法可以作为联系固体与液体真空应力张量的有效途径。

Abstract: The electromagnetic vacuum stress tensor in a system consisting of classical point-like atoms on a cubic lattice is derived directly from the electric field and, alternatively, using the known more phenomenological formula involving the (usually unknown) derivative of the dielectric tensor. The results agree. The stress tensor contains a nontrivial constant and therefore cannot be obtained from macroscopic electrodynamics alone.
  A system consisting of a mosaic of randomly oriented grains of a cubic crystal is used as a model of an amorphous solid. The electromagnetic stress tensor in this system is deduced by averaging the corresponding quantity in a cubic crystal over all orientations. Two components remain: the component in the direction of the electric field and the component perpendicular to it. The transverse component agrees with the analogous quantity for liquids derived by Peierls in an entirely different way.

</details>


### [128] [Reusable Operators for Irreducible Cartesian Tensor Decomposition and Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05971)
*Mingjian Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用的、可复用的构造方法，用于将任意给定内禀对称性的张量分解为不可约笛卡尔张量（ICT），并显式给出提取与重构算子，同时应用于弹性张量及等变机器学习中的笛卡尔谐波和 ICT 耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量的分子与材料性质在旋转下的行为可通过对张量进行不可约分解来显式化。秩二分解是教科书结果，但对高秩张量以及具有内禀对称性的物理张量，分解并不平凡。与成熟的球谐形式体系相比，笛卡尔形式长期以来缺乏一种针对给定内禀对称性的通用构造，以及可复用、能精确提取和重构 ICT 的算子。

Method: 作者发展了一种通用构造，显式求得提取 ICT 与精确重构原张量的算子。这些算子仅依赖于张量的秩和对称性，因此每个类只需构建一次即可应用于该类任意张量。在此基础之上，进一步得到向量的笛卡尔谐波，以及将两个 ICT 耦合成第三个 ICT 的算子。方法在二阶（秩四）和三阶（秩六）形式的弹性张量上进行了演示，并利用秩四张量的 ICT 定义了旋转与尺度不变的各向异性度量，在 Materials Project 的晶体弹性张量上进行了评估。整体构造已在开源软件包 natto 中实现，可生成精确符号形式和数值形式的算子。

Result: 成功开发出仅依赖秩与对称性的可复用提取/重构算子，并在此基础上获得笛卡尔谐波与 ICT 耦合算子；对弹性张量（秩四和秩六）完成了演示，定义了旋转与尺度不变的各向异性度量，并在 Materials Project 数据集上进行了量化评估；所有算子均可在 natto 中以精确符号与数值两种形式生成。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了笛卡尔形式下高秩、含内禀对称性张量不可约分解通用构造的空白，提供了一次构建、反复使用的提取与重构算子，并延展到等变机器学习所需的笛卡尔谐波和 ICT 耦合，为张量性质分析与各向异性度量提供了实用且开源的实现。

Abstract: Molecular and material properties, from the polarizability to the elastic constants, are described by tensors. Their behavior under rotations is made explicit when a tensor is decomposed into irreducible parts that transform independently. In Cartesian form these parts are the symmetric and traceless irreducible Cartesian tensors (ICTs), whose decomposition and coupling underlie selection rules, orientational averages, and the use of symmetry. The decomposition is a textbook result at rank two, but higher rank and the intrinsic symmetry of physical tensors make it nontrivial. What has been lacking, unlike in the well-established spherical formalism, is a general construction for a given intrinsic symmetry, together with reusable operators that extract the ICTs and rebuild the original tensor exactly. Here, we develop such a construction and obtain these operators explicitly. These operators depend on rank and symmetry alone, and therefore each need only be built once and then applied to any tensor of that class. Building on them, we further obtain the Cartesian harmonics of a vector and the operators that couple two ICTs into a third, both central to equivariant machine learning. The construction is demonstrated on the elastic tensor, in both its second-order form of rank four and its third-order form of rank six. The ICTs of the rank-4 tensor also define a rotation- and scale-invariant measure of anisotropy, which we evaluate across the first-principles elastic tensors of crystalline materials from the Materials Project. The construction is implemented in the open-source package natto, which produces the operators in both exact symbolic and numerical form.

</details>


### [129] [Oxidation of Tantalum Nano-Film by Microwave Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06019)
*Massimiliano Zamengo,Lina Grineviciute,Hsin-Hui Huang,Haoran Mu,Julianija Nikitina,Saulius Juodkazis,Junko Morikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对比了三种将200 nm钽薄膜氧化为Ta2O5的方法——飞秒激光直写、高温管式炉退火与微波腔退火，发现微波退火最快（数分钟、约50 W），并利用氧化前后介电常数剧变导致的腔谐振频率与品质因子变化，实现原位、非侵入式实时监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高效、快速且可实时监控的钽纳米薄膜氧化方法，突破传统高温炉退火耗时长的局限，并寻找能原位表征金属-氧化物转变的非侵入式诊断手段。

Method: 采用三条路线氧化200 nm钽膜：(i) 飞秒激光（515 nm/200 fs）直写，在单个焦斑900次脉冲的强重叠下、平均单脉冲通量约0.1 J/cm2的窄窗口内实现氧化而不烧蚀；(ii) 600 °C管式炉高温退火；(iii) 2.45 GHz微波腔退火（约50 W）。同时监测微波腔谐振频率与品质因子Q在金属-氧化物转变时的变化。

Result: 管式炉需600 °C、1小时才能将200 nm Ta完全转化为409 nm Ta2O5，而微波退火仅需数分钟（约50 W）。飞秒激光在约0.1 J/cm2的平均单脉冲通量下启动氧化，且受烧蚀阈值上限约束形成窄窗口。微波退火中，腔谐振频率与Q值在金属-氧化物转变时显著改变，反映2.45 GHz下介电常数实部与虚部的塌缩。

Conclusion: 微波退火是比高温炉更高效的Ta纳米膜氧化方式；金属-氧化物转变产生的显著介电对比度使微波腔本身成为传感器，可通过谐振频率漂移实时跟踪氧化进程，提供非侵入式原位诊断工具。

Abstract: Oxidation and ablation of 200 nm tantalum films were carried out by three routes: (i) femtosecond (fs-)laser direct write, (ii) high-temperature annealing (HTA) in a tube furnace, and (iii) annealing in a 2.45 GHz microwave cavity. Complete conversion of the 200 nm Ta layer into 409 nm of Ta2O5 required one hour at 600 C in the furnace, but only minutes at ~ 50 W of microwave power. Fs-laser (515 nm/200 fs) oxidation of the Ta nano-film set in at an average single-pulse fluence of ~ 0.1 J/cm2 under strong pulse-to-pulse overlap (900 pulses per focal spot), i.e. within a narrow window bounded from above by the onset of ablation. Under microwave annealing, both the cavity resonance frequency and the quality factor Q changed markedly at the metal-to-oxide transition, reflecting the collapse of the real and imaginary parts of the permittivity at 2.45 GHz. This dielectric contrast turns the cavity into a sensor: the oxidation can be followed in real time from the shift of the cavity resonance, providing a non-invasive, in-situ diagnostic tool.

</details>


### [130] [Elucidating Guest-Host Mechanisms in ZIF-L for Tuneable Highly Luminescent 2D Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06028)
*Dylan A. Sherman,Lorenzo Donà,Cyril Besnard,Lars Mester,Ben Slater,Judit Farrando-Pérez,Christopher Allen,Joaquín Silvestre-Albero,Jin-Chong Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了2D金属有机框架ZIF-L作为发光染料宿主，通过调控合成条件实现可调的发光性能，开发出高量子产率、高光稳定性的guest@ZIF-L发光材料，并展示了白光发射及柔性薄膜原位制备的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D MOFs作为发光客体宿主已较为成熟，但2D guest@MOF体系研究较少；2D体系因光学透明性、层间距和超薄特性具有独特优势，因此亟需阐明客体在ZIF-L中的嵌入机制并验证2D架构在功能发光方面的优越性。

Method: 以荧光素@ZIF-L(F@ZIF-L)为案例，采用纳米尺度FTIR、衍射和拓扑映射确认荧光素位于ZIF-L框架空腔中；结合表面能模拟揭示客体嵌入程度与形貌连续演变的关系；通过调节合成温度和溶剂比例，调控客体负载量及单体/聚集体比例；并研究了苝@ZIF-L的白光发射及在柔性锌箔上原位生长取向发光薄膜。

Result: 优化后的F@ZIF-L实现了可调的发射色度、99.7%的光致发光量子产率、12个月溶液中的极低客体浸出及高光稳定性；Perylene@ZIF-L表现出独特白光发射（CIE坐标0.33, 0.34），源于黄色α相激基缔合物与蓝色单体发射的组合；成功在锌箔上原位生长出取向的guest@ZIF-L发光薄膜。

Conclusion: 发光染料@ZIF-L体系在开发可调谐、高韧性的发光元件方面潜力巨大，有望应用于下一代光电子器件、传感器及照明系统。

Abstract: 3D metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) are well known effective hosts for luminescent guests that improve tuneability, photostability and material fabricability. 2D guest@MOF systems, while less explored, offer competitive advantages over 3D guest@MOF systems due to their optical transparency, inter-layer spacing, and vertical thinness. This work examines luminescent organic dyes@ZIF-L to establish the underlying mechanisms of guest incorporation in ZIF-L and demonstrate the advantages of the 2D ZIF-L architecture for the material's functional luminescence. Analysing a case study system, fluorescein@ZIF-L (F@ZIF-L), using nanoscale FTIR, diffraction, and topology mapping, confirmed that fluorescein (F) resided in the ZIF-L framework cavities. Supported by surface energy simulations, the extent of guest incorporation was found to be indicated by a morphological continuum, from the characteristic leaf-shaped ZIF-L to rectangular F@ZIF-L. By modifying synthesis temperature and solvent ratios, the luminescent properties of F@ZIF-L could be rationally tuned in terms of guest loading (% mol) and arrangement (i.e. guest monomer to aggregate ratio). When optimised, F@ZIF-L exhibited tuneable emission chromaticity, 99.7% photoluminescent quantum yield, minimal guest leaching in solution over 12 months, and high photostability. Perylene@ZIF-L exhibited unique white light emitting properties, with CIE coordinates (0.33, 0.34) arising from a combination of yellow alpha-phase excimer and blue monomeric perylene emission. Finally, oriented luminescent thin films of guest@ZIF-L materials were grown on malleable Zn foils, demonstrating an in situ fabrication technique. Together, the work highlights the potential for luminescent dye@ZIF-L systems in developing tuneable and resilient luminescent components of next-generation optoelectronics, sensors, and lighting systems.

</details>


### [131] [Revealing the origin of ionic conduction in silver-iodide-doped silver phosphate glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06738)
*Jennifer Freedberg,Joseph Maduzia,Andias Santoso,Ranveer Singh,Placid Ferreira,Fahad Mahmood*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过对原型玻璃态电解质 (AgI)x(AgPO3)(1-x) 进行时域太赫兹光谱测量，揭示了介于低频输运响应与高频束缚极化之间的介观频率区间，并发现有离子输运从束缚电流主导到短程色散输运的交叉转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快离子传输是许多固体电解质的特征，但其微观起源尚不完全清楚；缺乏微观认识导致下一代固态电池的开发主要依赖经验。现有测量大多只能获取低频输运响应或高频束缚极化，而介观频率区才是离子输运真正涌现的区域，因此需要填补这一观测空白。

Method: 改变 AgI 浓度 x，对原型玻璃态电解质 (AgI)x(AgPO3)(1-x) 进行时域太赫兹光谱（TDTS）测量，以揭示介观频率区间并识别传导机制的交叉转变。

Result: 揭示了介于低频与高频之间的介观频率区间，并识别出从束缚电流主导的传导到由短程色散离子输运引起的电导率的交叉。P-O⁻-Ag⁺ 基元的键弯曲运动所关联的束缚极化在所有组分中均存在，但仅凭其本身不足以产生离子输运；只有该极化嵌入足够软的 AgPO3 玻璃基质并伴随高载流子密度时，输运才得以涌现，使体系从振动束缚响应转变为短程色散运动。

Conclusion: P-O⁻-Ag⁺ 键弯曲极化与柔软玻璃基质、高载流子密度共同作用，是使玻璃态电解质从振动束缚响应过渡到短程色散离子输运的必要条件，为理解快离子传输的微观机制提供了新依据。

Abstract: Fast ionic transport is a defining feature of many solid electrolytes, yet its microscopic origin is not fully understood. In the absence of microscopic insights, the development of next-generation solid-state batteries remains largely empirical. Most existing measurements access either the low-frequency transport response or the high-frequency bound polarization, yet the intermediate mesoscopic frequency regime is where ionic transport emerges. By varying the $\mathrm{AgI}$ concentration ($x$) and performing time-domain terahertz spectroscopy (TDTS) in a prototypical glassy electrolyte $\left(\mathrm{AgI}\right)_{x}\left(\mathrm{AgPO_3}\right)_{(1-x)}$, we reveal this intermediate frequency regime and identify a crossover from bound-current-dominated conduction to conductivity arising from short-range dispersive ionic transport. We find that bound polarization associated with the bond-bending motion of the $\mathrm{P{-}O^- -Ag^+}$ motif is present across compositions but is insufficient to produce ionic transport on its own. Transport emerges only when this polarization is embedded in a sufficiently soft $\mathrm{AgPO_3}$ glassy matrix and accompanied by a high carrier density. These ingredients together take the system from a vibrationally bound response to short-range dispersive motion.

</details>


### [132] [How to enhance the applicability of Murnaghan equation of state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07400)
*V. V. Bannikov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对固体等温状态方程进行综述，并针对广泛使用的Murnaghan方程适用范围有限的问题，提出一种简单高效的改进方案，使该方程能够同时可靠描述近平衡区与高压区，并通过从头计算及多种固体的实验V-P数据验证了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Murnaghan等温状态方程虽被广泛使用，但其适用范围有限，无法同时可靠地再现近平衡区和高压区的数据，因此需要一种简单而有效的方法加以改进。

Method: 作者提出一种对Murnaghan方程的修正方法，通过改进其形式以增强高压下的适用性，从而显著扩大对实验或计算数据可靠再现的范围；并用从头计算得到的U(V)数据以及代表性固体的实验V-P数据进行拟合测试，与普通Murnaghan方程及其他常用方程（Vinet、Birch-Murnaghan、Mao）进行对比。

Result: 改进后的Murnaghan方程在从头计算数据拟合中表现出显著优势，可将可靠再现范围从平衡态扩展到远高于普通方程所能达到的压力；在实验数据拟合方面，改进方程在所列实例中至少不逊于其他常用状态方程。文中还讨论了拟合过程中的若干问题及获得该状态方程不同形式的替代途径。

Conclusion: 所提出的改进Murnaghan方程能有效扩展其高压应用范围，在计算数据再现方面明确优于原方程，并在实验数据拟合中与Vinet、Birch-Murnaghan、Mao等方程相当或更优，是一种简单而实用的状态方程改进方案。

Abstract: The paper presents the brief overview of known isothermal equations of state for solids and of theoretical background to construct them, focusing on widely used Murnaghan equation. Since the latter has a limited range of application, failing to reproduce reliably both near-equilibrium and high-pressure regions simultaneously, the paper proposes simple, but rather efficient way to improve it. The suggested modification favors its applicability at high pressures, allowing to expand considerably the region of reliable reproducing of experimental or computational data. The proposed improved equation has been tested in fitting of U(V) data, obtained within ab initio computations, as well as of experimental V-P data for a representative selection of solids. In comparison with ordinary Murnaghan equation, it has found significant advantages, extending the range, where the computational data may be reproduced trustworthy (from the equilibrium state to the pressures, appreciably higher, the ordinary equation could). As for the fitting of experimental data, the proposed improved Murnaghan equation reproduces them, in the presented examples, at least not worse than other well-known equations (Vinet, Birch-Murnaghan, Mao) do. Some aspects of fitting process, as well as some alternative approaches to obtain the proposed equation of state in various forms are also discussed.

</details>


### [133] [Momentum-Resolved Electronic Structure for Quasicrystals: Full-Band Spectra and Chern Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06043)
*Donglin Yang,Huajie Chen,Dexuan Zhou,Xiaoxu Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在倒空间中系统收敛的紧束缚框架，用于研究准晶的全带谱与拓扑性质，通过傅里叶模散射通道哈密顿量与逐级扩张的赝布里渊区相结合，实现了从局域量到体热力学量的精确转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶缺乏平移对称性，导致布洛赫分解和布里渊区积分方法失效，使得全带的、动量分辨的电子结构难以表述和计算；现有低能有效模型无法覆盖全带行为。

Method: 开发一个倒空间紧束缚框架，包含两个可系统细化的组成部分：一是傅里叶模散射通道哈密顿量，在每个物理动量处给出局域的谱量及流-流关联量；二是一个逐级扩张的赝布里渊区层级，通过精确的局域-全局关系将局域量转换为体热力学可观测量。

Result: 应用于彭罗斯（Penrose）和安曼-比恩克（Ammann-Beenker）模型，揭示了全带动量分辨的准能带，以及赝隙形成的多通道机制，均超出低能有效模型的范畴；进一步解析了塞曼驱动的能隙关闭与重开、量子化的陈数平台，以及体谱与拓扑可观测量的相位子（phason）不变性。

Conclusion: 该框架为准晶的全带谱与拓扑性质提供了一条统一的倒空间路径，突破了低能有效模型的局限，具有系统收敛性。

Abstract: Quasicrystals lack the translational symmetry that underlies Bloch decomposition and Brillouin-zone integration, making full-band momentum-resolved electronic structure difficult to formulate and compute. We develop a systematically convergent reciprocal-space tight-binding framework for a broad class of quasicrystals. The method combines two systematically refinable components: a Fourier-module scattering-channel Hamiltonian that yields local spectral and current-current correlation quantities at each physical momentum, and an expanding hierarchy of pseudo-Brillouin zones that converts the resulting local quantities into bulk thermodynamic observables through an exact local-to-global relation. Applied to the Penrose and Ammann-Beenker models, the framework uncovers full-band momentum-resolved quasibands and a multichannel mechanism for pseudogap formation, both beyond the scope of low-energy effective models. It further resolves Zeeman-driven gap closings and reopenings, quantized Chern plateaus, and the phason invariance of bulk spectral and topological observables. This framework provides a unified reciprocal-space route to full-band spectral and topological properties of quasicrystals.

</details>


### [134] [Water, vacancies, and competing exchange interactions in Prussian blue analogues: a neutron diffraction study of field and dehydration-driven magnetic transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08976)
*N. S. Dhami,C. V. Colin,V. Nassif,O. Fabelo,T. Nait,A. Bleuzen,A. Bordage,V. Balédent*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过中子衍射系统研究了普鲁士蓝类似物(PBAs)家族的结构与磁性质,揭示了空隙相关缺陷/间隙水的内在漫散射特征、脱水引发的正-负热膨胀转变,以及MnFe化合物中由磁场或脱水驱动的自旋重取向,并将其归因于反铁磁Mn-Fe耦合与受挫Mn-Mn相互作用的近补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清MnFe基PBAs在Fe K边XMCD响应中长期存在的解释歧义,并阐明水含量对这类材料结构稳定与磁稳定的关键作用,为其作为电池电极的应用提供基础。

Method: 对CoFe、MnFe、NiFe及Rb2NiFe、MnCr等多个PBA样品进行了温度(2-450 K)和磁场依赖的中子衍射研究;结合阳离子稳定的Rb2NiFe骨架对比识别缺陷相关漫散射;采用极简Heisenberg模型分析磁耦合与受挫,并与XMCD及脱水实验对照。

Result: 所有Fm-3m族双金属化合物在低散射角呈现与关联空隙及间隙水相关的宽漫散射特征;脱水使CoFe和MnFe出现正-负热膨胀连续转变而NiFe保持结构稳定;低温下除MnFe外均形成k=(0,0,0)共线亚铁磁序,MnFe呈部分受挫的k=(1,0,0)结构;Bc=1.3 T磁场或脱水均可诱导MnFe自旋重取向至k=(0,0,0);Heisenberg模型表明该转变源于Mn-Fe反铁磁耦合与fcc Mn子晶格上几何受挫的Mn-Mn反铁磁相互作用近补偿,使MnFe处于磁补偿点附近。

Conclusion: 研究解决了MnFe基PBAs的XMCD解释歧义,确立了水含量同时控制结构稳定与磁稳定的关键地位,相关认识直接关系到该类材料作为电池电极的应用。

Abstract: We report a neutron diffraction study of the structural and magnetic properties of a family of ferro- and ferrimagnetic Prussian blue analogues (PBAs), $A_4$[Fe(CN)$_6$]$_{2.7}$ ($A$ = Co, Mn, Ni), Rb$_2$Ni$_4$[Fe(CN)$_6$]$_{3.3}$, and Mn$_4$[Cr(CN)$_6$]$_{2.7}$, as a function of temperature (2--450~K) and applied magnetic field. All bimetallic compounds of the Fm$\overline{3}$m family exhibit a broad diffuse feature at low scattering angle, which we identify, through comparison with the cation-stabilized Rb$_2$NiFe framework, as an intrinsic signature of correlated vacancies and their associated interstitial water. High-temperature diffraction reveals a continuous crossover from positive to negative thermal expansion in CoFe and MnFe upon dehydration, while NiFe remains structurally robust up to 450~K. At low temperature, all compounds order in a collinear ferrimagnetic state with propagation vector $\mathbf{k}=(0,0,0)$, except MnFe, which adopts a partially frustrated magnetic structure with $\mathbf{k}=(1,0,0)$. A moderate magnetic field of $B_c = 1.3$~T drives a spin reorientation in MnFe toward the collinear $\mathbf{k}=(0,0,0)$ ferrimagnetic state common to the other compounds; the same transition is independently induced by dehydration. A minimal Heisenberg model shows that this transition results from a near-compensation between antiferromagnetic Mn--Fe coupling and a geometrically frustrated antiferromagnetic Mn--Mn interaction on the face-centered-cubic Mn sublattice, placing MnFe in the vicinity of a magnetic compensation point. These results resolve a longstanding ambiguity in the interpretation of the Fe $K$-edge XMCD response of MnFe-based PBAs, and establish water content as a key parameter controlling both the structural and magnetic stability of this family of materials, with direct relevance to their use as battery electrodes.

</details>


### [135] [Altermagnetism from the viewpoint of chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06159)
*Nayana Devaraj,Anumita Bose,Md Afsar Reja,Arka Bandyopadhyay,Awadhesh Narayan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文从化学视角系统综述了交替磁性（altermagnetism）这一新兴磁有序类型，涵盖其基本概念、材料家族、实验表征、计算材料发现、潜在应用以及挑战与展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性研究长期以化学组成与键合为核心，交替磁体作为兼具铁磁体和反铁磁体特征的新磁有序类型，其产生源于特定的对称性与电子结构。然而，从晶体化学和化学键合角度系统理解与发现交替磁体仍缺乏清晰的化学驱动框架，亟需一个以化学为导向的综述来串联概念、材料、表征与应用。

Method: 本综述采用化学驱动的方法论：（1）从对称性、轨道特征与电子结构信号出发介绍交替磁体的基本概念；（2）综述已发现交替磁性的各类材料家族，聚焦配位环境与结构-性能关系；（3）介绍用于表征交替磁体材料的实验方法；（4）探讨以对称性和键合等化学约束指导计算搜索的从头算材料发现策略。

Result: 综述梳理了交替磁性在不同材料家族中的实现，阐明了有利于交替磁序的配位环境与结构-性能关系；总结了有效的实验表征手段；展示了以化学约束引导从头算搜索发现新交替磁体的潜力，并揭示了其与铁电性和超导电性等既有序的耦合及技术应用前景。

Conclusion: 交替磁体兼具本征科学价值与技术应用潜力，可与其他有序（如铁电、超导）耦合以拓展功能。未来需建立基于化学的设计准则来应对挑战、推动该领域发展；化学驱动的策略有望成为推进交替磁性研究的关键路径。
