Abstract: Magnetism has been a central theme of research in chemistry, physics, and materials science, with chemical composition and bonding playing key roles in determining magnetic behavior. Altermagnets are a newly identified class of magnetic materials that combine features of conventional ferromagnets and antiferromagnets, arising from specific symmetry and electronic structure motifs. In this review, we present a chemistry-driven viewpoint on altermagnetism, highlighting how crystal chemistry, bonding, and electronic structure enable this unconventional magnetic order. We begin by introducing the fundamental concepts required to understand altermagnets, with an emphasis on symmetry considerations, orbital character, and electronic structure signatures. We then survey the diverse material families in which altermagnetism has been identified, drawing attention to coordination environments and structure-property relationships that favor altermagnetic order. We subsequently present experimental approaches which are useful for the characterization of altermagnetic materials. We examine ab initio materials discovery as a promising strategy for identifying new altermagnets, emphasizing how chemical constraints, such as symmetry and bonding, can guide computational searches. Other than their intrinsic importance, altermagnets provide interesting possibilities for technology. For this reason, we highlight possible applications that may be enabled through altermagnetic materials, along with their coupling with existing orders such as ferroelectricity and superconductivity. In conclusion, we point out some challenges and prospects, where chemically-based design guidelines can play an important role towards advancing altermagnetism research. In summary, this review offers an account of recent developments in altermagnetism, from basic concepts to the current state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [136] [A Transport Framework for Evaluating Nanoscale Interconnect Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06170)
*Md. Rafiqul Islam,Patrick E. Hopkins*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个结合热导率、电导率和传输优值（归一化与非归一化）的传输评估框架，用于在纳米尺度约束下定量比较替代金属互连材料的传输性能，并应用于 Cu、Ru、W、Co、Ir、Mo 薄膜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 CMOS 技术持续微缩，金属互连因电阻率升高、自热效应和可靠性退化而日益限制电路性能，但缺乏在纳米尺度约束下定量评估替代金属传输性能的统一框架。

Method: 提出一个传输框架，将独立测量的热导率和电导率与归一化及非归一化传输优值相结合；对 Mo、Co、Ir 薄膜进行新的稳态热反射（SSTR）测量，并结合已有 Cu、Ru、W 数据，对六种金属进行评估。

Result: Cu 表现出最高的本征传输性能，但其有效性能因厚度缩放和 Ta 衬垫层电阻而显著下降；Ru 和 Mo 在保持良好传输性能的同时可实现无阻挡层集成，被识别为下一代 CMOS 互连的有前景候选材料。

Conclusion: 本文提出的传输框架能够有效评估纳米尺度下替代互连金属的性能，指出 Cu 的尺寸效应劣势，并推荐 Ru 和 Mo 作为下一代 CMOS 互连的优选材料。

Abstract: As CMOS technology continues to scale, metallic interconnects increasingly limit circuit performance through rising resistivity, self-heating, and reliability degradation. Although several alternative metals have been proposed, a quantitative framework for evaluating their transport performance under nanoscale confinement remains unavailable. Here, we establish a transport framework by combining independently measured thermal and electrical conductivities with normalized and unnormalized transport figures of merit. We apply this framework to Cu, Ru, W, Co, Ir, and Mo thin films using new steady-state thermoreflectance measurements of Mo, Co, and Ir together with previously reported Cu, Ru, and W data. While Cu exhibits the highest intrinsic transport performance, its effective performance is substantially reduced by thickness scaling and Ta liner resistance. In contrast, Ru and Mo maintain favorable transport properties while enabling barrierless integration, identifying them as promising candidates for next-generation CMOS interconnects

</details>


### [137] [Zonal dislocations in Laves phases: A coupled synchro-shear slip mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06198)
*Sang-Hyeok Lee,Mariano Forti,Thomas Hammerschmidt,Gang Liu,Julien Guénolé,Siyuan Zhang,Gerhard Dehm,Sandra Korte-Kerzel,Zhuocheng Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过原子模拟和高分辨扫描透射电子显微镜，发现了C15 NbCr2等Laves相中一种新型塑性变形机制——耦合同步剪切滑移，并揭示其在Laves多型相变和孪生行为中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laves相在高温下的主要塑性变形机制是同步剪切，由同步Shockley不全位错（即zonal位错）通过扭折对形核与扩展等局域化事件介导。然而，现有的同步剪切机制认识不足，难以解释Laves多型之间的相变以及孪生行为，因此需要在原子尺度上厘清更复杂的滑移与位错协同机制。

Method: 采用原子模拟（atomistic simulations）识别新型位错滑移机制，并结合高分辨扫描透射电子显微镜（HR-STEM）对C15 NbCr2的扩展位错核心结构进行实验表征与验证。

Result: 发现了一种新型滑移机制——耦合同步剪切滑移，即两条同步Shockley不全位错在相邻滑移面上同步滑移，形成外禀层错（extrinsic stacking faults）。HR-STEM观测到C15 NbCr2中由外禀层错界定的耦合同步Shockley不全位错扩展核心结构，并证实其参与孪生过程。

Conclusion: 耦合同步剪切滑移在Laves多型之间的相变以及孪生行为中起着关键作用，为理解拓扑密堆金属间化合物塑性变形的动力学本质提供了新的原子尺度认识。

Abstract: Synchro-shear is the primary plastic deformation mechanism in Laves phases at elevated temperatures, mediated by synchro-Shockley partial dislocations-zonal dislocations that proceed via localized events such as kink-pair nucleation and propagation. Using atomistic simulations, we identified a novel slip mechanism in Laves phases, namely coupled synchro-shear slip, involving the synchronized glide of two synchro-Shockley partial dislocations on adjacent slip planes, leading to the formation of extrinsic stacking faults. High-resolution scanning transmission electron microscopy revealed the extended core structures consistent with coupled synchro-Shockley partial dislocations bounded by extrinsic stacking faults in C15 NbCr2 and their involvement in twinning. These results highlight the critical role of coupled synchro-shear slip in enabling phase transformations between Laves polytypes and in governing twinning behavior, providing new atomistic insight into the kinetic nature of plasticity in topologically close-packed intermetallic phases.

</details>


### [138] [Chemical and interface confinement effects in promoting plastic co-deformation in high-strength nano-scale eutectics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06334)
*Arkajit Ghosh,Amit Misra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究激光快速凝固的Al-(Si,Ge)共晶复合材料，利用三元单变共晶路径设计成分与组织层级，发现富( Si,Ge )纤维在塑性变形中保持抗裂并发生变形诱导平面缺陷，而Ge在Al相中偏析至亚晶界束缚位错，由此实现金属相与共价相的协同变形，为开发高强度高延性层级共晶材料提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质相异质结构中，金属相（fcc Al）与共价相（dc Si/Ge）因塑性与滑移系性质差异大，存在塑性不兼容问题，传统仅靠长度尺度调控强化难以兼顾强度与延性；本文旨在通过共晶组织细化与相内化学/晶体学层级耦合来克服这一矛盾。

Method: 采用激光快速凝固沿Al-Si-Ge三元单变共晶路径制备复合材料，形成富Al面心立方(fcc)基体与富(Si,Ge)金刚石立方(dc)纤维构成的共晶相，界面平直（faceted）。通过原位SEM微力学测试结合事后STEM与TEM表征，系统分析不同Ge含量下的共晶间距、屈服强度、拉伸延性及变形机制，观察Al相中亚晶界Ge偏析、位错受限，以及(Si,Ge)纤维中变形诱导平面缺陷与局部部分位错活动。

Result: 组织呈层级特征：Al相内含纳米级(Si,Ge)团簇，(Si,Ge)纤维内存在生长孪晶并保留部分Al。增加Ge含量虽使共晶间距粗化，但屈服强度较更细的Al-Si略有提升，且Al-(Si,Ge)的拉伸延性高于Al-Si。变形中，Al相内Ge偏析至变形诱导亚晶界限制滑移位错；同时富(Si,Ge)纤维在大塑性应变下保持抗裂并出现变形诱导平面缺陷，与高应力界面及孪晶界处的局部部分位错活动一致。

Conclusion: 界面约束与硬相化学的双重耦合机制促使金属相与共价相协同变形，突破了传统长度尺度控制强化的局限，为设计高抗拉强度兼具延性的层级共晶材料提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Eutectics offer a route to overcome plastic incompatibility in disparate-phase heterostructures by coupling microstructural refinement with phase-specific chemical and crystallographic hierarchy. Here, we investigate laser-rapid-solidified Al-(Si,Ge) eutectic composites designed along the univariant ternary eutectic path, solidifying with an Al-rich face-centered cubic (fcc) matrix and (Si,Ge)-rich diamond-cubic (dc) fibers as constituent eutectic phases with faceted interfaces. The microstructure was hierarchical in nature with finer structures within eutectic phases: nanoscale (Si,Ge) clusters in the Al phase and growth twins within the (Si,Ge) fibers with some Al retention. Although increasing Ge content coarsens the eutectic spacing, the yield strength is slightly enhanced compared to the relatively finer Al-Si, and tensile ductility of Al-(Si,Ge) is higher than that of Al-Si. In situ SEM micromechanical testing combined with post-mortem STEM and TEM showed that in the Al-rich phase, Ge segregated to the deformation-induced sub-grain boundaries that confined glide dislocations. Simultaneously, the (Si,Ge)-rich fibers remain crack-resistant at large plastic strain and exhibit deformation-induced planar faults, consistent with localized partial-dislocation activity at highly stressed interfaces and twin boundaries. These coupled mechanisms due to interface confinement and hard phase chemistry enable co-deformation of the metallic and covalent phases, providing a pathway for designing high-tensile-strength and ductile hierarchical eutectics beyond conventional length-scale-controlled strengthening.

</details>


### [139] [First Principles Thermodynamics of Zr B Segregation at Grain Boundaries in Recycled Nd2Fe14B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06335)
*Avik Mahata,Miha Zakotnik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用自旋极化DFT+U计算，从第一性原理角度解释了微量Zr和B在回收Nd2Fe14B永磁体晶界及三叉晶界处的优先偏聚及其热力学来源，并说明这种晶界局部化配置如何支撑高矫顽力回收磁体的微结构稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回收Nd2Fe14B永磁体中仅恢复硬磁相并不能重建晶间微结构，而实验发现即使Zr总浓度仅约0.1 at.%，纳米ZrB2析出物仍会在晶界和三叉晶界出现，并伴随晶界稳定化和晶粒粗化抑制。然而这种优先局域化的原子尺度热力学机理尚不清楚，需要第一性原理加以阐明。

Method: 采用自旋极化、含Hubbard校正（U）的密度泛函理论（DFT+U）计算。构建一系列成分匹配的体相/晶界DFT+U超胞，在体相与晶界两种环境中系统比较B、Zr、Dy以及ZrB2-like局域构型的热力学稳定性，并分析磁性状态（Fe亚晶格磁矩与归一化磁化强度）。

Result: 过量B在晶界处被强烈稳定化；Zr也独立地表现出对界面区域的热力学偏好；Zr与B以ZrB2-like构型结合后仍保持晶界偏好，说明随Zr-B配位发展界面依然有利。磁性分析表明所有比较结构处于相同的高磁矩Fe亚晶格状态，且晶界局域缺陷对归一化磁化强度的扰动总体上小于其体相对照。

Conclusion: 计算结果确立了Zr与B在ZrB2形成前共同局域化的热力学路径，为Zr-B化学在晶间区域富集提供了第一性原理解释；并说明这种晶界局域化状态能够支撑高矫顽力回收Nd-Fe-B磁体所需的微结构稳定性。

Abstract: Grain-boundary chemistry is central to the coercivity and thermal stability of Nd2Fe14B permanent magnets, particularly in recycled magnets where recovery of the hard-magnetic phase does not by itself restore the intergranular microstructure. Our recent experimental study showed that nanoscale ZrB2 precipitates can emerge at grain boundaries and triple junctions during recycling despite an overall Zr concentration of only about 0.1 at.%, where they are associated with boundary stabilization and suppression of grain coarsening. Here we use spin-polarized density-functional theory with a Hubbard correction (DFT+U) to determine the atomistic thermodynamics underlying this preferential localization. A systematic set of composition-matched bulk/grain-boundary DFT+U supercells provides a common correlated-electron description across boundary chemistries. We compare B, Zr, Dy, and ZrB2-like local configurations in bulk and grain-boundary environments of Nd2Fe14B. Excess B is strongly stabilized at the boundary, while Zr also shows an independent thermodynamic preference for the interfacial region. When Zr and B are combined in a ZrB2-like configuration, the boundary preference is retained, indicating that the interface remains favorable as Zr-B coordination develops. These results establish a thermodynamic pathway for the co-localization of Zr and B prior to ZrB2 formation. Magnetic-state analysis further shows that all compared structures remain within the same high-moment Fe-sublattice regime, and boundary-localized defects generally perturb the normalized magnetization less than their matrix counterparts. The calculations therefore provide a first-principles explanation for why Zr-B chemistry concentrates at intergranular regions and how such boundary-localized states can support the microstructural stability required for high-coercivity recycled Nd-Fe-B magnets.

</details>


### [140] [Barrierless Water Dissociation on Rare-Earth Sesquioxide Surfaces from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06350)
*Shuxiang Zhou,Jay A. LaVerne,Hanna Hlushko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用结合从头算与在线机器学习力场加速的分子动力学，系统研究了水在三种立方方铁锰矿型稀土倍半氧化物（Sc₂O₃、Y₂O₃、Lu₂O₃）(110)表面上的解离，识别出两条不同解离路径：具有约0.1 eV小能垒的近端机制，以及此前未报道、几乎无能垒且在吸附与解离阶段均能量占优的远端机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水在金属氧化物表面的解离是多相催化、光催化和辐射化学中的关键基元步骤，但其在稀土倍半氧化物上的机理细节仍缺乏深入理解，尤其是静态计算方法难以揭示动态、无偏的反应图景。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟，将从头算与在线机器学习力场加速相结合；对每种材料采样25条独立轨迹，在Sc₂O₃、Y₂O₃和Lu₂O₃三种立方方铁锰矿型氧化物的(110)表面研究水的解离过程。

Result: 识别出两条不同的解离路径：一是传统近端机制，能垒较小但有限（约0.1 eV）；二是此前未报道的远端机制，几乎无能垒，且在吸附和解离两个阶段均能量占优。低能垒归因于方铁锰矿晶格中周期性排列的固有欠配位RE³⁺位点。

Conclusion: 有序的固有配位缺陷在稀土倍半氧化物中扮演了与传统氧化物中随机氧空位类似的作用，为理解该类材料表面的水解离及催化行为提供了新的机理视角。

Abstract: Water dissociation on metal oxide surfaces is a key elementary step in heterogeneous catalysis, photocatalysis, and radiation chemistry, yet its mechanistic details on rare-earth (RE) sesquioxides remain poorly understood. Here, we investigate water dissociation on the (110) surfaces of three cubic bixbyite oxides, Sc$_2$O$_3$, Y$_2$O$_3$, and Lu$_2$O$_3$, using molecular dynamics combining ab initio calculations with on-the-fly machine-learning force field acceleration. By sampling 25 independent trajectories per material, we obtain an unbiased picture of the reaction landscape inaccessible to conventional static calculations. Two distinct dissociation pathways are identified: a conventional proximal mechanism with a small but finite barrier of $\sim$0.1 eV, and a previously unreported distal mechanism that is effectively barrierless and energetically preferred at both the adsorption and dissociation stages. The low barriers are consistent with the periodic array of inherently undercoordinated RE$^{3+}$ sites in the bixbyite lattice, suggesting that ordered intrinsic coordination defects play a role analogous to stochastic oxygen vacancies in conventional oxides.

</details>


### [141] [First-Principles Study of Magnetism, Electronic Structure, and Bonding in Nb-Mn-Ge Kagome Compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06362)
*Wei-Shen Tee,Shiya Chen,Weiyi Xia,Peter Minch,Cai-Zhuang Wang,Vladimir Antropov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，系统研究了预测稳定的 NbMn6Ge6、NbMn6Ge5 和 NbMn6Ge4 的磁性基态、电子结构与成键特性。结果表明：NbMn6Ge6 为共线反铁磁，而菱方结构的 NbMn6Ge5 和 NbMn6Ge4 偏好铁磁态；Mn 上磁矩几乎局域化；AFM NbMn6Ge6 与 FM NbMn6Ge5 呈单轴磁各向异性，FM NbMn6Ge4 为面内各向异性；费米面附近未发现适合拓扑磁性研究的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统探究计算预测稳定的 NbMn6(Ge6/Ge5/Ge4) 系列材料的磁性基态、磁各向异性、电子结构与成键性质，为可能的拓扑磁性研究及后续实验合成提供理论依据。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，结合磁构型比较确定磁基态；通过电荷密度差与电子局域函数分析揭示电荷重分布与成键特征；并考察费米面附近的电子结构以评估拓扑磁性可能性。

Result: 结构稳定的 NbMn6Ge6 呈共线反铁磁基态；亚稳的菱方 NbMn6Ge5 与 NbMn6Ge4 为铁磁基态。Mn 磁矩近乎局域，可用广义自旋哈密顿量描述。AFM NbMn6Ge6 与 FM NbMn6Ge5 具有单轴磁各向异性，FM NbMn6Ge4 具有面内各向异性。电荷密度差与 ELF 分析显示 Mn-Ge Kagome 网络内及相邻层间存在电荷重分布与成键特征。费米面附近电子结构不具备拓扑磁性研究的特征。

Conclusion: 这三类 Nb-Mn-Ge 化合物具有明确的磁性基态与磁各向异性，但费米面附近的电子结构不适合拓扑磁性研究；相关预测需要通过实验合成、结构表征和磁性测量加以验证。

Abstract: In this work, we systematically investigate the magnetic ground states, electronic structures, and bonding characteristics of the computationally predicted stable NbMn6Ge6, NbMn6Ge5, and NbMn6Ge4 using first-principles calculations. Our results show that structurally stable NbMn6Ge6 has a collinear antiferromagnetic configuration, while the metastable rhombohedral NbMn6Ge5 and NbMn6Ge4 favor ferromagnetic ground states. Magnetic moments on Mn atoms are nearly localized, suggesting the applicability of a generalized spin Hamiltonian. Magnetic anisotropy in AFM NbMn6Ge6 and FM NbMn6Ge5 has uniaxial behavior, while FM NbMn6Ge4 has in-plane anisotropy. Charge density difference and electron localization function analyses further show charge redistribution and bonding features within the Mn-Ge Kagome network and between adjacent structural layers. Electronic structures near the Fermi level show no features suitable for topological magnetism studies. Experimental synthesis, structural characterization, and magnetic measurements are required to verify our predictions.

</details>


### [142] [$β-(Al_{0.19} Ga_{0.81})_2 O_3/Ga_2 O_3$ Modulation-Doped Field-Effect Transistors with $>$ 6 kV Breakdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06414)
*Julian Gervassi Saga,Joshua T Buontempo,Nabasindhu Das,Advait Gilankar,Hari P Nair,Nidhin Kurian Kalarickal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种集成高质量Al2O3栅介质与SiNx钝化的β-(Al0.19Ga0.81)2O3/Ga2O3调制掺杂场效应晶体管（MODFET），实现了6.6 kV的击穿电压——这是该类MODFET迄今报道的最高值，功率品质因数达222 MW/cm²，凸显其在多kV功率器件中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向下一代多千伏级功率器件的需求，需要兼具高击穿电压、低导通电阻与优异开关性能的器件结构。β-Ga2O3因超宽禁带和高临界电场而备受关注，但如何通过调制掺杂异质结构进一步提升β-(AlxGa1-x)2O3/Ga2O3 MODFET的耐压能力与综合性能，是亟待突破的关键问题。

Method: 制备β-(Al0.19Ga0.81)2O3/Ga2O3调制掺杂场效应晶体管，并集成高质量Al2O3栅介质和SiNx钝化层以实现高压工作。通过在Flourinert中开展三端击穿测试，系统研究不同栅漏间距（LGD）对击穿特性的影响，并提取平均横向电场与功率品质因数。

Result: 器件实现最大漏极电流54 mA/mm、导通电阻149 Ω·mm、最小亚阈值斜率94 mV/dec、开关比超过10^8。击穿电压随栅漏间距增大而提升，在LGD=28 μm时达到6.6 kV，对应平均横向电场接近2.4 MV/cm，功率品质因数达222 MW/cm²。

Conclusion: 所演示的6.6 kV击穿电压是β-(AlxGa1-x)2O3/Ga2O3 MODFET迄今报道的最高值，验证了调制掺杂β-Ga2O3异质结构结合高质量栅介质与钝化技术，在下一代多kV级功率器件领域的巨大应用潜力。

Abstract: $β-(Al_{0.19} Ga_{0.81})_2 O_3/Ga_2 O_3$ modulation-doped field-effect transistors (MODFETs) incorporating a high-quality Al2O3 gate dielectric and SiNx passivation are demonstrated for high-voltage operation. The devices exhibited a maximum drain current of 54 mA/mm, an on-resistance of 149 Ohm.mm, a minimum subthreshold slope of 94 mV/dec, and an ION/IOFF ratio exceeding $10^8$. Three-terminal breakdown measurements performed in Flourinert demonstrated a breakdown voltage that increased with gate-drain spacing, reaching 6.6 kV for a device with LGD = 28 microns. The corresponding average lateral electric field approached 2.4 MV/cm, while a power figure of merit of 222 MW/cm2 was achieved. To the best of the authors knowledge, the demonstrated breakdown voltage represents the highest reported value for a $β-(Al_x Ga_{1-x})_2 O_3/Ga_2 O_3$ MODFET and highlights the potential of modulation-doped $β$-Ga2O3 heterostructures for next-generation multi-kV power devices.

</details>


### [143] [Machine learning for the design and prediction of soft-magnetic electromagnetic shielding FeCo-based alloys in laser cladding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06417)
*Luting Wang,Suiyuan Chen,Xiancheng Zhu,Zhiqing Fang,Mei Wang,Yifan Li,Lei Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出多种机器学习策略，基于实验推导与软磁材料数据库，在近乎无限的成分空间中加速设计兼具电磁屏蔽、磁学与电学性能的多功能FeCo基合金，并结合激光增材制造实现快速自动化发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁屏蔽材料在航空航天和日常生活中至关重要，但其设计与制造仍面临持续挑战；机器学习可加速材料开发与成分优化，激光增材制造则为制备多组分、多功能电磁屏蔽材料提供技术支持。

Method: 基于实验推导和软磁材料数据库，提出多种机器学习策略，在几乎无限的成分空间中加速设计多功能FeCo基电磁干扰屏蔽合金。

Result: 提出一种快速、自动化的多功能合金发现新方法，能够优化电磁干扰屏蔽效能，并提升磁性与电学性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为电磁屏蔽材料的快速自动化发现提供了新途径，展示了机器学习结合激光增材制造在多组分、多功能合金设计中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Electromagnetic shielding materials play a pivotal role in both aerospace applications and daily life. However, their design and manufacturing still face persistent challenges. Machine learning demonstrates significant potential in accelerating material development and compositions optimization. Furthermore, laser additive manufacturing provides powerful technical support for fabricating multi-component, multifunctional electromagnetic shielding materials with tailored properties. In this study, the multiple machine learning strategies have been proposed, based on experimental derivation and soft magnetic material databases, to accelerate the design of multifunctional FeCo-based alloys for electromagnetic interference (EMI) shielding within an almost infinite compositional space. This work presents a novel approach for the rapid and automated discovery of multifunctional alloys with optimized EMI shielding effectiveness, as well as enhanced magnetic and electrical properties.

</details>


### [144] [First-principles-based Prediction of Phase Fields: Part I. Binary and Ternary Refractory Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06627)
*Pravan Omprakash,Nicholas Crnkovich,John Cavin,Nathan Curtis,Adrien Couet,Rohan Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于DFT二元混合焓与亚规则溶液模型的高通量计算框架，用于预测Cr、Hf、Mo、Nb、Ta、Ti、V、W、Zr系难熔多主元合金（MPEA）的相图，无需拟合高阶相互作用，并在36个二元和15个三元相图上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难熔多主元合金（MPEA）相平衡复杂，涉及多元固溶体和金属间化合物；其巨大成分空间使第一性原理计算成本过高，而CALPHAD又受限于稀缺的实验数据，因此亟需一种可规模化、能高效预测温度-成分相图的框架。

Method: 该框架将DFT计算的二元混合焓与亚规则（sub-regular）溶液模型相结合来构建相图，不拟合高阶相互作用；通过亚规则溶液模型引入晶格依赖的能量学，并纳入温度依赖的元素相变，从而实现对溶线相界和相场的预测。

Result: 在36个二元和15个三元相图上验证，与实验结果和CALPHAD计算均吻合良好；引入晶格依赖能量学与温度依赖元素相变后预测精度提升。框架能够捕捉混溶间隙、固溶体稳定性和金属间化合物形成，预测的混溶温度通常在实验值的300 K以内。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条可扩展的、基于第一性原理的难熔MPEA相图高通量预测路径，并开发了公开可访问的网页界面（https://raptor.engr.wustl.edu）用于交互式探索预测相图。

Abstract: Multiple principal element alloys (MPEAs) exhibit complex phase equilibria involving multinary solid solutions and intermetallics, which makes it challenging to predict their temperature-composition phase diagrams. Their vast compositional space makes first principles methods prohibitively expensive, while CALPHAD is limited by scarce experimental data. Here, we present a computationally efficient framework to predict the solvus phase boundaries, and hence, phase fields, in refractory MPEAs composed of Cr, Hf, Mo, Nb, Ta, Ti, V, W, and Zr. The approach combines DFT calculated binary mixing enthalpies with sub regular solution models to construct phase diagrams without fitting higher order interactions, enabling efficient scaling across composition space. Validation against 36 binary and 15 ternary phase diagrams demonstrates good agreement, with both experimental results and CALPHAD calculations. We find that the prediction accuracy is enhanced by incorporating lattice dependent energetics through sub regular solution models and including temperature-dependent elemental phase transitions. The framework captures miscibility gaps, solid solution stability, and intermetallic formation, with predicted miscible temperatures typically within 300 K of experimental values. Overall, this work establishes a scalable, first principles based route for highthroughput prediction of phase diagrams in refractory MPEAs. A publicly accessible web interface has also been developed to allow interactive exploration of the predicted phase diagrams, available at https://raptor.engr.wustl.edu.

</details>


### [145] [Coupled structural and electronic evolution under pressure in CuIr2Se4, CuRh2S4, and CuRh2Se4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07106)
*M. Emi,M. Shiomi,K. Kojima,K. Sugimoto,T. Karasawa,H. Suzuki,M. Takahashi,K. Oka,H. Kadobayashi,S. Kawaguchi-Imada,N. Hirao,T. Ohashi,D. Ito,T. Kubo,M. Matsushita,M. Nohara,K. Matsubayashi,N. Katayama*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合同步辐射粉末X射线衍射和电阻率测量，研究了CuIr2Se4、CuRh2S4和CuRh2Se4三种尖晶石硫族化物在高压下的结构演化与输运性质，发现三者均形成相关的单斜超胞，结构相变与绝缘态输运的出现密切相关，并在CuIr2Se4中发现了常压下此前未报道的体超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖晶石硫族化物为研究金属态、超导态与压力诱导绝缘态之间的相互作用提供了平台，此前研究覆盖的压力范围有限，需要进一步拓展压力范围以厘清高压单斜相的形成与绝缘输运演化之间的关系，以及过渡金属和硫族元素取代如何调控特征压力尺度和与超导的竞争。

Method: 采用同步辐射粉末X射线衍射与电阻率测量相结合，在超出前人探索范围的压力区间内研究三种化合物；高压衍射揭示出三者均具有紧密相关的单斜超胞；对CuIr2Se4和CuRh2S4用基于密度泛函理论弛豫的结构模型进行约束精修，对CuRh2Se4则确定了相容的单斜晶胞但未解析原子尺度有序图样；同时通过零电阻和交流抗磁响应确认常压超导。

Result: 三种化合物在高压下均形成相关的单斜超胞，CuIr2Se4和CuRh2S4的结构符合Phase-IV型键歧化结构，CuRh2Se4仅确定了相容的单斜晶胞。CuIr2Se4的绝缘型输运在极窄压力范围内突变出现，而Rh基化合物则在更宽压力范围内逐渐出现，与其各自的结构转变密切对应。CuIr2Se4在0.29 K达到零电阻，交流抗磁响应接近完全超导屏蔽，表明存在常压体超导。

Conclusion: 高压单斜相的形成与向绝缘输运的演化之间存在紧密关联，过渡金属和硫族元素取代可在密切相关的结构框架内调节特征压力尺度及其与超导电性的竞争。

Abstract: Spinel chalcogenides provide a platform for investigating the interplay among metallic, superconducting, and pressure-induced insulating states. Here, we combine synchrotron powder X-ray diffraction and electrical-resistivity measurements to investigate the pressure evolution of CuIr2Se4, CuRh2S4, and CuRh2Se4 over pressure ranges extending beyond those previously explored. High-pressure diffraction reveals closely related monoclinic supercells in all three compounds. For CuIr2Se4 and CuRh2S4, constrained profile fits based on structural models relaxed using density functional theory are compatible with Phase-IV-type bond-disproportionated structures, whereas the data for CuRh2Se4 establish a compatible monoclinic unit cell without resolving its atomic-scale ordering pattern. Insulating-like transport develops abruptly over a narrow pressure range in CuIr2Se4 but more gradually over broader pressure ranges in the Rh-based compounds, in close correspondence with their respective structural transformations. We also establish previously unreported bulk superconductivity in CuIr2Se4 at ambient pressure: zero resistance is attained at 0.29 K, and the accompanying ac diamagnetic response is consistent with nearly complete superconducting shielding. These results establish a close relationship between the formation of the high-pressure monoclinic phases and the evolution toward insulating transport, and demonstrate that transition-metal and chalcogen substitutions tune the characteristic pressure scales and the competition with superconductivity within a closely related structural framework.

</details>


### [146] [Collective Excitonic Structure Governs Anomalously Weak Thermal Optical Dephasing in Conjugated Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06742)
*Henry J. Kantrow,Elizabeth Gutiérrez-Meza,Eric R. Bittner,Hao Li,Carlos Silva-Acuña*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用相干检测与布居检测两种二维电子光谱方法，研究了一系列半导体共轭聚合物的均匀光学线宽随温度的变化，发现尽管分子结构与固态组织差异巨大，所有聚合物的光学退相干均表现出极弱的温度标度，但线宽的绝对值取决于所测量的可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轭聚合物聚集体在强振动耦合下表现出光学退相干，且其化学结构、固态组织与激子特性高度多样；作者希望系统厘清这类体系中均匀光学线宽的温度依赖规律，并考察不同检测方式对测量结果的影响。

Method: 采用相干检测（coherence-detected）与布居检测（population-detected）两种二维电子光谱（2DES）技术，对一系列半导体共轭聚合物测量均匀光学线宽随温度的变化，并对比两种检测模态的差异。

Result: 尽管聚合物在分子结构和固态组织上差异巨大，所有被测聚合物的热致线宽标度都异常微弱；但均匀线宽的绝对值因检测可观测量不同而不同——相干检测与布居检测分别以不同方式将布居弛豫和纯退相投影到光谱响应上。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了光学退相干在一系列多样化共轭聚合物中具有弱热标度这一普遍规律，并指出其实验表现必须在检测可观测量的语境下加以解读。

Abstract: Conjugated polymer aggregates exhibit optical decoherence in the presence of strong vibronic coupling and substantial diversity in chemical structure, solid-state organization, and excitonic character. Here, we use coherence-detected and population-detected two-dimensional electronic spectroscopies to examine the temperature dependence of the homogeneous optical linewidth across a series of semiconducting polymers. Despite substantial differences in molecular architecture and solid-state organization, all polymers studied exhibit remarkably weak thermal linewidth scaling over the measured temperature range. Comparison between complementary detection modalities further shows that, while this weak temperature dependence is obust, the absolute homogeneous linewidth depends on the measured observable. This behavior reflects the different ways in which coherence- and population-detected measurements project population relaxation and pure dephasing onto the spectroscopic response. These results establish the weak thermal scaling of optical decoherence across a diverse series of conjugated polymers and show that its experimental manifestation must be interpreted in the context of the detection observable.

</details>


### [147] [A Novel Nb-Based Eutectic Superalloy with Exceptional Ultrahigh-Temperature Mechanical Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06848)
*Ayeman M. Nahin,Alfredo Navarrete,Xiaokun Yang,Eric A. Lass,Mingwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种可铸造的铌基共晶超合金 NMC-1（Nb-10Mo-9.5C，原子百分比），具有 Nb-Mo 固溶体与 Nb2C 碳化物的均匀层片结构，在 1300 °C 和 1500 °C 下屈服强度分别约 300 MPa 和 200 MPa，兼具应变硬化、非零室温拉伸塑性和优异的热稳定性，其比强度-成本综合指标远超商用 Nb、Mo、Ta、W 合金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有难熔合金无法同时满足 1300 °C 以上服役所需的高温强度、热稳定性、低密度、低成本以及室温拉伸塑性，急需一种能兼顾各项性能的设计新范式。

Method: 设计并制备一种可铸造的 Nb 基共晶超合金 NMC-1（Nb-10Mo-9.5C，at.%），形成 Nb-Mo 固溶体与 Nb2C 碳化物组成的均匀层片（lamellar）结构，并系统表征其高温力学性能、热稳定性、密度与成本。

Result: NMC-1 在 1300 °C 和 1500 °C 的 0.2% 屈服强度分别约 300 MPa 和 200 MPa，为已报道 Nb 基合金中的最高水平之一；具有明显的应变硬化、非零室温拉伸塑性；在 1400 °C 保温 100 h 后未观察到组织粗化；密度 8.65 g/cc，成本约 $85/kg，比强度-成本优异指数远超商用 Nb、Mo、Ta、W 合金。

Conclusion: NMC-1 通过共晶层片结构实现了高温强度、热稳定性、低密度、低成本与室温塑性的协同，建立了超高温结构材料的新设计范式。

Abstract: Refractory alloys operating above 1300 °C, beyond the limit of Ni-based superalloys, must balance high-temperature strength, thermal stability, low density, low cost, and room-temperature tensile ductility, a combination that existing refractory alloys have yet to achieve. Here we report a castable Nb-based eutectic superalloy, Nb-10Mo-9.5C (at. %, NMC-1), comprising a uniform lamellar structure of Nb-Mo solid solution and Nb2C carbide phases. NMC-1 achieves 0.2% yield strengths of ~300 MPa and ~200 MPa at 1300 °C and 1500 °C, respectively, among the highest reported for Nb-based alloys, with pronounced strain hardening, non-zero room-temperature tensile ductility, and no observed microstructural coarsening after 100 h at 1400 °C. Its low density (8.65 g/cc) and cost (~$85/kg) yield a specific-strength-per-cost merit index that far surpasses commercial Nb, Mo, Ta, and W alloys, establishing a new design paradigm for ultrahigh-temperature structural materials.

</details>


### [148] [Magneto-spin-orbit graphene: Chern topology and photoinduced Hall response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06858)
*Artem V. Tarasov,Alexander V. Eryzhenkov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算与有效四带模型，研究四种面外磁化石墨烯/（Au、Pt）/（Co、Mn）异质结在公度(2×2)与莫尔(9×9)超胞中的陈绝缘相，揭示莫尔尺度堆叠与埋层界面缺陷对拓扑及光诱导霍尔响应的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁-自旋轨道耦合石墨烯可通过邻近效应产生陈相，但实际异质结中这些效应依赖于衬底化学、局部堆叠序，并可能因埋层界面重构而改变；而这类异质结常出现的长程莫尔条纹使相关效应难以准确刻画，需专门方法处理。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算与有效四带模型，对四种面外磁化的Gr/(Au,Pt)/(Co,Mn)异质结，分别在公度(2×2)和莫尔(9×9)超胞中建模；其中Co基界面进一步引入失配位错以考察埋层界面重构的影响。

Result: 公度胞高估电荷转移并夸大谷不对称性，而莫尔平均显著减弱两者同时保持可观的自旋劈裂；Pt间隔层比Au介导更强的邻近相互作用，Co换Mn以依赖间隔层的方式改变有效交换相互作用平衡。失配位错选择性调节Gr/Au/Co的狄拉克能隙或Gr/Pt/Co的自旋劈裂。Gr/Pt/Co在所有超胞中均呈现有效铁磁陈相（C=2），而其余三种组分的莫尔模型为亚铁磁且拓扑平庸（C=0），但仍表现出螺旋度依赖的光诱导霍尔响应，且在Gr/Au/Co中因失配位错重构而显著增强。

Conclusion: 莫尔尺度堆叠控制有效陈相与霍尔电导，而埋层界面缺陷在不改变拓扑的前提下调节光诱导霍尔响应，证明了两类结构因素对石墨烯邻近效应异质结性质的分工式调控。

Abstract: Magneto-spin-orbit graphene can host Chern phases due to proximity interactions, but in realistic heterostructures these depend on substrate chemistry, local stacking registry and may be altered by buried-interface reconstruction. Investigation of these effects requires special efforts to take into account the long-range moire patterns that often arise for these heterostructures. We address this question using first-principles calculations and effective four-band models for four out-of-plane magnetized Gr/(Au, Pt)/(Co, Mn) heterostructures in commensurate (2 x 2) and moire (9 x 9) supercells where the latter include misfit dislocations for the Co-based interfaces. The commensurate cells overestimate charge transfer and exaggerate valley asymmetry, whereas moire averaging strongly reduces both effects while preserving sizable spin splittings. The Pt spacer mediates stronger proximity interactions than Au, while replacing Co by Mn substantially changes the balance of the effective exchange interactions in a spacer-dependent manner. Introduction of misfit dislocations into the Co-based structures is selective and tunes the Dirac gap in Gr/Au/Co or spin splittings in Gr/Pt/Co. Graphene in Gr/Pt/Co exhibits effective ferromagnetic Chern phase with C = 2 in all studied supercells. By contrast, the moire models of the other three compositions are ferrimagnetic and topologically trivial, with C = 0. Nevertheless, they exhibit helicity-dependent photoinduced Hall responses, which are strongly enhanced by misfit-dislocation reconstruction in Gr/Au/Co. This work demonstrates that moire-scale stacking controls the effective Chern phase and Hall conductivity, whereas buried-interface defects tune the photoinduced Hall response without changing the topology.

</details>


### [149] [Human-agent discovery of reconfigurable in-plane ferroelectric superdomain control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06887)
*Yu Liu,Boris Slautin,Ching-Che Lin,Jaegyu Kim,Lane W. Martin,Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARC框架，让编码智能体与人类操作员共享显微镜、笔记本及两个记忆文件（FINDINGS.md与PITFALLS.md），用于扫描探针的探索性自动化实验。该框架被用于重构(111)取向PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3薄膜的面内超畴方向，成功实现方向选择，并以光栅扫描结合掩模脉冲晶格在超畴取向中"打印"出UTK字母。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化等自动化实验要求实验开始前就定义好可观测变量、可用操作和优化目标。但许多探索性实验中，描述样品的变量需从数据中提取、新操作在实验中涌现、仪器预算太小不足以靠反复试错学习问题，因此需要一种能在实验过程中动态定义变量与操作、并从失败中学习的框架。

Method: 提出SPARC框架：编码智能体（coding agent）与人类操作员共享一台显微镜、一个笔记本和两个持久记忆文件——FINDINGS.md存储对实验的分级结论，PITFALLS.md记录已学到的分析与仪器使用的失败模式。先在操作员监督的实验中，智能体重分析早前手动测量数据并开发出交替极性的定向静止偏压脉冲晶格来重构超畴方向；随后在智能体自主控制的实验中，将PITFALLS.md条目编译为写入前校验设计方案的检查规则。通过空间极性交替（而非晶格与层状周期的精确匹配）实现方向选择，并用光栅扫描与掩模脉冲晶格结合实现图案化。

Result: 实验表明，决定方向选择的是空间极性交替，而非晶格周期与层状周期的精确匹配。通过光栅扫描与掩模脉冲晶格叠加，成功将"UTK"字母打印进超畴取向。该过程还明确了智能体实验的实用需求：对仪器执行进行物理验证、存储结论保持有效的条件、在仪器数据上验证新可观测变量，以及鲁棒的控制协议。

Conclusion: SPARC框架通过持久记忆文件与代码生成式智能体的协作，实现了探索性扫描探针实验的自动化，并能从失败中积累经验并先验校验设计。该框架不仅能定向控制铁电超畴，还能将任意图案写入材料取向，同时揭示了智能体自动化实验所需的四项关键实用要求，为更广泛的探索性科学自动化提供了范式。

Abstract: Automated experimentation is most effective when the observables, available actions, and objective are defined before the experiment starts, as is the case for Bayesian optimization. However, in many exploratory experiments, the variables that describe the sample must be extracted from the data, new operations emerge during the experiments, and the instrument budget is too small to learn the problem by trials. Here we introduce the Scanning Probe Agentic Research Cycle (SPARC) framework, in which a coding agent and a human operator share one microscope, one notebook, and two persistent memory files. FINDINGS.md stores graded conclusions about the experiment, whereas PITFALLS.md records learned failure modes of analysis and instrument. We apply SPARC to reconfigure the in-plane superdomain direction of a (111)-oriented PbZr0.2Ti0.8O3 film. In an operator-supervised campaign, the agent reanalyzed earlier manual measurements and developed an oriented lattice of stationary bias pulses with alternating polarity to reconfigure the superdomain direction. In a subsequent agent-controlled campaign, PITFALLS.md entries were compiled into checks that validate a design before any write. The experiments showed that spatial polarity alternation, instead of the exact matching between the lattice and lamellar periods, determines directional selection. Combining a raster scan with a masked pulse lattice printed the letters UTK into the superdomain orientation. The campaign also identified practical requirements for agentic experimentation where physical verification of instrument execution, the conditions under which stored findings remain valid, validation of new observables on instrument data, and robust control protocols.

</details>


### [150] [Scalable machine learning framework for multiphase identification from powder X-ray diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06908)
*Xinyang Tong,Ethan Jin,Jiahan Xu,Aditya Rao,Pengcen Jiang,Nathan J. Szymanski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GALAXI 将XRD物相鉴定解耦为独立的“一对一”二分类器，先缩小候选相范围，再用Rietveld精修确定最佳物相组合，在实验图谱上取得0.935的micro-F1分数，优于经典搜索匹配和已有深度学习模型，并可扩展到6万余个COD结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XRD是合成后鉴定晶相的主要工具，但自动化物相鉴定仍具挑战性，尤其对于存在峰重叠和实验伪影的多相样品。现有深度学习方法多将物相鉴定建模为单一封闭集分类问题，需要一个共享模型区分所有候选相，难以扩展到大型参考库。

Method: GALAXI将物相鉴定解耦为独立的“一对多”二分类器，每个分类器专精于识别单一物相；这些预训练分类器先将搜索空间缩小到少量可信物相，再通过Rietveld精修评估这些物相，找出最能解释完整衍射图谱的物相组合。该方法为模块化架构，可扩展至COD的64,594个结构，并通过公开网页接口部署。

Result: 在精选的实验图谱集上，GALAXI识别正确物相的micro-F1分数达0.935，优于经典搜索匹配和已有深度学习模型；对低杂质相含量、小晶粒尺寸、峰位移、样品位移、织构等常见实验伪影保持稳健，并在固相反应的时间分辨原位XRD数据上表现良好。

Conclusion: GALAXI通过模块化、“一对多”二分类器加Rietveld精修的架构，在保持高精度的同时实现了无需重训即可扩展到大型参考库的能力，为自动化、可扩展的XRD物相鉴定提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: X-ray diffraction (XRD) is the primary tool for identifying crystalline phases following synthesis, but automated phase identification remains challenging, particularly for multiphase samples with overlapping peaks and experimental artifacts. While deep-learning methods have been proposed to improve upon classical search-match algorithms, most formulate phase identification as a single closed-set classification problem, requiring one shared model to discriminate among all candidate phases. Here we introduce GALAXI, which instead decouples the identification task into independent one-versus-all binary classifiers that each specialize in recognizing a single phase. These pre-trained classifiers first narrow the search space to a small set of plausible phases, which are then evaluated through Rietveld refinement to identify the combination of phases that best explains the full diffraction pattern. On a curated set of experimental patterns, GALAXI identifies the correct phases with a micro-F1 score of 0.935, outperforming classical search-match and prior deep-learning models. The method remains robust to common experimental artifacts, including low impurity phase fractions, small crystallite size, peak shifts, sample displacement, and texture, and performs well when applied to time-resolved in-situ XRD data from solid-state reactions. Moreover, because the phase-specific models are independent, GALAXI can expand to large reference libraries without retraining existing models. This modular architecture enables us to train classifiers for 64,594 structures from the Crystallography Open Database and deploy them through a public web interface at https://galaxi-xrd.com.

</details>


### [151] [Restoring the Surface Magnetic Gap in MnBi$_2$Te$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06915)
*Ce Bian,Hengxin Tan,Wenhui Duan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文揭示MnBi₂Te₄表面磁能隙消失（无能隙态）的起源：表面缺陷降低静电势，将拓扑表面态推入次表层，抑制交换相互作用从而闭合能隙；通过外电场或范德华异质结构中的界面场调控表面静电势可恢复能隙并控制其拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征磁性拓扑绝缘体MnBi₂Te₄中量子反常输运虽被广泛预言，但实验实现受阻于其难以捉摸的表面磁能隙（常表现为无能隙态）。要利用其拓扑性质，必须先阐明无能隙态的起源。

Method: 结合模型计算与实验验证：理论研究表明表面缺陷降低静电势、驱使拓扑表面态离域至次表层并抑制交换相互作用，从而关闭能隙；通过外电场或范德华异质结构中的界面场调控表面静电势可恢复能隙。实验上在缺陷MnBi₂Te₄薄膜与极性绝缘体构成的异质结构中验证了该机制，并解释了近期实验中AlOx覆盖层增强量子反常霍尔效应的现象。

Result: 调控表面静电势可恢复预期的表面磁能隙并实现对其拓扑性质的控制；缺陷MnBi₂Te₄/极性绝缘体异质结构实验验证了该理论，解释了AlOx覆盖下量子反常霍尔效应增强的观测结果。

Conclusion: 静电驱动的表面态离域是能隙被抑制的一个竞争性起源；提出通过位移场工程实现稳健的量子化输运，为MnBi₂Te₄中量子反常输运的实验实现提供途径。

Abstract: A widespread experimental realization of quantized anomalous transport in the intrinsic magnetic topological insulator MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ is hindered by its elusive surface magnetic gap. Uncovering the origin of the gapless states is essential for accessing its topological properties. Here we show that surface defects lower the electrostatic potential, drive topological surface states into subsurface layers, suppress exchange interactions, thereby closing the gap. Tuning the surface electrostatic potential via external electric fields or interfacial fields in van der Waals heterostructures restores the expected gap and enables control of its topology. This is confirmed by model calculations and validated in defective MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ films interfaced with polar insulators, explaining the enhanced quantum anomalous Hall effect under AlO$_x$ capping observed in recent experiments. Our theory identifies electrostatically driven surface-state delocalization as a competitive origin of gap suppression and proposes displacement-field engineering for robust quantized transport.

</details>


### [152] [Metastable polar order and phase competition in Carpy-Galy LaTaO4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06943)
*Yongjin Shin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算，作者在Carpy-Galy LaTaO4的反极性P21/c基态与极性Cmc21相之间，识别出一个亚稳的极性单斜P21相作为相变路径中的公度中间态，并阐明了其由序参量耦合与Gamma_1+弛豫稳定的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LaTaO4在反极性P21/c基态与极性Cmc21相之间存在非公度调制区间，但缺乏一个对称性上可将二者连接的中间参考相，限制了对其相变路径和极性-反极性竞争机制的理解。

Method: 作者利用第一性原理（DFT）计算，通过模式分解（mode-resolved）能量面分析、晶格度量比较和中子衍射对比，研究P21/c与Cmc21之间的对称性破缺路径、序参量耦合及Gamma_1+弛豫的作用，并考察了组成依赖的能量学。

Result: 亚稳的极性单斜P21相是P21/c和Cmc21的共同子群，提供了对称性连通的相变路径；P21/c→P21转变具有有限能垒，受限软模模型在一段简谐刚度范围内保留P21与Cmc21两个竞争能谷；计算所得公度态与实验IC-o调制的S_2+主导位移特征一致，且组成依赖的能量学显示可通过化学手段调控极性-反极性竞争。

Conclusion: P21可作为连接LaTaO4反极性基态与极性相的对称性合法的公度参考态，S_2+主导的位移特征支持其与IC-o调制的关联，为通过化学组成调控LaTaO4体系的极性-反极性竞争提供了依据。

Abstract: Carpy-Galy LaTaO4 undergoes a structural sequence linking the antipolar monoclinic P21/c ground state and the polar orthorhombic Cmc21 phase through an incommensurately modulated regime. First-principles calculations identify a metastable polar monoclinic P21 phase as a commensurate intermediate in the phase-transition landscape. As a common subgroup of P21/c and Cmc21, P21 provides a symmetry-connected route between the established polymorphs. Mode-resolved energy surfaces for these symmetry-breaking pathways show that coupling between the respective primary order parameters and an isosymmetric Gamma_1+ relaxation stabilizes the P21 minimum along both pathways. Consequently, the P21/c -> P21 transformation has a finite energy barrier, while a restricted soft-mode model retains competing P21 and Cmc21 basins over an illustrative range of harmonic stiffness. Lattice-metric and neutron-diffraction comparisons indicate a shared S_2+-dominated displacement character between the calculated commensurate state and the experimental IC-o modulation, supporting the use of P21 as a commensurate structural reference for IC-o. Finally, composition-dependent energetics suggest chemical routes for tuning polar-antipolar competition.

</details>


### [153] [Quantum hot carrier spectra in plasmonic catalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06949)
*Yu Chen,Hanwen Jin,Fei Gao,Johannes Lischner,Shiwu Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用原子级描述研究银纳米颗粒上氧分子的振动激发与解离，发现热载流子的量子化分布随粒径减小而增强，提升振动耦合与解离效率，振动激发速率呈1/D线性标度且高光强下非线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面分子在金属纳米颗粒上的振动活化是等离激元催化的基本步骤，但量子尺度下由热载流子驱动的动力学机制尚未完全理解。

Method: 采用等离激元热载流子产生的原子级描述，系统研究不同直径D下银纳米颗粒上氧气的振动激发与解离，考察从经典尺度到量子尺寸区间的变化。

Result: 随D减小进入量子尺寸区间，热载流子出现量子化分布，高能区布居增加；高能热载流子驱动更高效的振动耦合与解离。振动激发速率呈现线性1/D标度（源于朗道阻尼），在高光强下因多重电子散射引起的振动加热转为非线性。

Conclusion: 等离激元催化中热载流子量子化分布的发现，为选择性调控与非热能量转换开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Vibrational activation of admolecules on metal nanoparticles is an elementary step in plasmonic catalysis, yet the underlying dynamics driven by hot carriers is not fully understood in the quantum regime. Using an atomistic description of plasmonic hot carrier generation, we investigate vibrational excitation and dissociation of oxygen on silver nanoparticles as a function of diameter D. As D reduces from the classical to quantum-sized regime, quantized distribution of hot carriers emerges with increasing population in the high-energy regions. These highly energetic hot carriers deliver more efficient vibrational coupling and dissociation. The rate of vibrational excitation shows a linear 1/D scaling, which results from Landau damping. It turns nonlinear at elevated light intensities due to vibrational heating generated by multiple electron scattering. The finding of quantized distribution of hot carrier in plasmonic catalysis opens new avenues for selective control and nonthermal energy conversion.

</details>


### [154] [Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory with Coulomb Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08983)
*Asbjørn Bækgaard Lauritsen,Mathieu Lewin,Jakob Oldenburg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次在连续介质中对含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）给出了完全严格的数学论证：给定一个含时密度，证明存在一个（在相差一个仅依赖于时间的常数意义下）唯一的外势，使得对应的薛定谔方程复现该密度，且该外势可由迭代格式得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDDFT 是量子多体计算的重要方法，但其核心的“密度到外势”的映射（Runge-Gross 型定理）此前缺乏在连续空间情形下的严格数学证明。作者旨在为 TDDFT 提供首个完全严格的量子力学基础，克服以往证明依赖时间泰勒展开、或仅适用于格点/离散模型的局限。

Method: 作者构造了一个迭代格式，通过对给定含时密度反解出对应的外势，并证明该迭代收敛。证明中避免了时间维度的泰勒展开，转而要求密度与外势在空间上具有解析性；相互作用允许为库仑相互作用，并兼容延展核（extended nuclei）的设定。

Result: 证明了外势的存在性与唯一性（唯一性至多相差一个仅依赖于时间的常数），并给出了可通过迭代算法实际获得该外势的构造性方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续介质中的 TDDFT 提供了严格的数学基石，表明在不依赖时间泰勒展开、仅在空间解析性假设下，密度到外势的反演问题可解且唯一，从而支撑 TDDFT 在含时量子体系中的可靠应用。

Abstract: We provide the first fully rigorous justification of time-dependent density functional theory in the continuum: Given a time-dependent density, we prove that there exists an external potential, unique up to a time-dependent constant, such that the corresponding Schrödinger equation reproduces the given density. This potential can be obtained with an iteration scheme. Our argument covers Coulomb interactions and does not need any Taylor expansion in time. It instead requires analyticity in space for the density and potential, which is compatible with extended nuclei.

</details>


### [155] [Excitons probe intrinsic flat band Mottness in a van der Waals heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07101)
*Xinyue Huang,Xintong Tan,Haowei Chen,Yingzhou Huang,Yushen Zhou,Yuchen Gao,Zhijie Ma,Chengxin Xiao,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jianpeng Liu,Zuxin Chen,Youguo Shi,Wang Yao,Yu Ye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合栅压可调光谱与第一性原理计算，研究单层WSe₂与vdW Mott绝缘体Nb₃Cl₈接触界面的激子行为，揭示强关联平带电子对激子的极化子修饰、Mott能隙重构及谷选择耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子是探测二维材料电子关联的灵敏光学探针，但其与内禀平带Mott体系的耦合尚未被充分探索，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 作者采用栅压可调光学光谱与第一性原理计算相结合的方法，研究单层WSe₂与范德华Mott绝缘体Nb₃Cl₈直接接触体系在栅压调控、电子掺杂及垂直磁场条件下的激子共振演化。

Result: 实验发现：1）Nb₃Cl₈中存在关联重构的Mott能隙，该能隙在单粒子能带图像中缺失；2）电子掺杂下WSe₂的2s里德堡激子经历多阶段演化，形成层间吸引与排斥极化子分支，表明里德堡激子可被相邻Mott层的强关联平带电子“修饰”；3）垂直磁场下自旋极化的Nb₃Cl₈态诱导谷选择激子耦合，显著增强WSe₂激子发射的圆偏振度。

Conclusion: 该工作将基于激子的传感与激子-极化子物理拓展到内禀平带Mott材料，为光学探测和工程调控关联驱动的界面准粒子提供了新途径。

Abstract: Excitons provide a sensitive optical probe of electronic correlations in nearby two-dimensional materials, yet their coupling to intrinsic flat-band Mott systems remains largely unexplored. Here we combine gate-tunable optical spectroscopy with first-principles calculations to study monolayer WSe$_2$ in direct contact with the van der Waals Mott insulator Nb$_3$Cl$_8$. The gate evolution of WSe$_2$ excitonic resonances reveals signatures of a correlation-reconstructed Mott gap in Nb$_3$Cl$_8$ that is absent from the single-particle band picture. In the electron-doped regime, the WSe$_2$ 2s Rydberg exciton undergoes a multistage evolution and develops into interlayer attractive and repulsive polaron branches, showing that a Rydberg exciton can be dressed by strongly correlated flat-band electrons in an adjacent Mott layer. Under an out-of-plane magnetic field, spin-polarized Nb$_3$Cl$_8$ states further induce valley-selective exciton coupling, producing a strongly enhanced circular polarization of the WSe$_2$ exciton emission. These results extend exciton-based sensing and exciton-polaron physics to intrinsic flat-band Mott materials, providing an optical route to probe and engineer correlation-driven interfacial quasiparticles.

</details>


### [156] [Groove-shaped defects in as-grown (001)-oriented $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ epilayers prepared by halide vapor phase epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07209)
*Yongzhao Yao,Daiki Katsube,Hirotaka Yamaguchi,Yukari Ishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过同步辐射X射线形貌术和电子显微镜，研究了卤化物气相外延生长的(001)取向β-Ga₂O₃同质外延层中沟槽状缺陷（GSDs）的形貌、亚表面结构及形成机制，发现GSDs并非由衬底位错成核，而是与晶片曲率引起的局部表面取向变化及阶梯供给有关，并伴随生长扇区合并产生的平面缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沟槽状缺陷（GSDs）会破坏β-Ga₂O₃外延层的表面平整度，迫使器件制造前进行化学机械抛光，从而增加加工成本和损伤风险，因此需要厘清GSDs的形成机制以从源头加以抑制。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线形貌术（透射模式）结合电子显微镜（SEM/TEM），表征GSDs的宏观形貌、晶片尺度分布、亚表面结构与缺陷特征；将表面图像与透射X射线形貌图进行空间对准以排查GSDs与衬底缺陷的对应关系；并通过TEM选区分析平面缺陷的α条纹衬度和晶面归属。

Result: GSDs沿[010]方向延伸数毫米，主要由(-102)基面刻面及陡峭的(100)侧壁构成；其晶片尺度分布与晶片曲率变化在空间上对应，表明局部表面取向影响GSD形成。表面图像与透射X射线形貌图的对准未发现GSDs与衬底缺陷的一一对应关系，排除衬底位错作为其成核位点。TEM在刻面化GSD扇区与周围(001)生长区交汇的终端边界附近发现平面缺陷，而在GSD中部试样中未观察到；这些缺陷呈现倾斜平移型平面缺陷特有的α条纹衬度，主要段落归属于(1-21)面。

Conclusion: 局部表面取向变化与阶梯供给的变化可能促进三维刻面化生长，形成持久的(-102)/(100)扇区；局部平面缺陷是生长扇区合并的后果，而非GSD成核的起因。研究揭示了晶片曲率和表面阶梯供给在GSD形成中的作用。

Abstract: Groove-shaped defects (GSDs) degrade the surface flatness of as-grown (001)-oriented $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ epilayers and necessitate chemical mechanical polishing before device fabrication, increasing processing costs and the risk of damage. We investigated the morphology, subsurface structure, and formation mechanism of GSDs in a homoepitaxial layer grown by halide vapor phase epitaxy using synchrotron X-ray topography and electron microscopy. The GSDs extended several millimeters along [010] and consisted predominantly of (-102) basal facets bounded by steep (100) sidewalls. Their wafer-scale distribution showed a spatial correspondence with variations in wafer curvature, suggesting that local surface orientation influences GSD formation. Careful alignment of surface images with transmission X-ray topographs revealed no one-to-one correspondence between GSDs and substrate defects, providing no evidence that substrate dislocations serve as their nucleation sites. Instead, transmission electron microscopy revealed planar defects localized near the terminal boundaries where the faceted GSD sectors met the surrounding (001) growth region; no such defects were observed in specimens extracted from the middle of GSDs. These defects exhibited α-fringe contrast characteristic of inclined translational planar defects, with the dominant segments assigned to the (1-21) plane. The observations suggest that variations in local surface orientation and step supply may promote three-dimensional faceted growth, producing persistent (-102)/(100) sectors. The localized planar defects are therefore interpreted as consequences of growth-sector coalescence rather than the origins of GSD nucleation. These findings provide insight into the roles of wafer curvature and surface step supply in GSD formation.

</details>


### [157] [Polymorph Engineering of the Layered Rare-Earth Magnet GdAlGe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08601)
*Dmitry V. Averyanov,Ivan S. Sokolov,Alexander N. Taldenkov,Oleg E. Parfenov,Alexey N. Mihalyuk,Ivan A. Yakovlev,Igor A. Karateev,Oleg A. Kondratev,Andrey M. Tokmachev,Vyacheslav G. Storchak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过相工程（polymorph engineering）制备了GdAlGe的一种此前被忽视的层状多晶型外延薄膜，该材料由三角形Gd晶格与蜂窝状AlGe晶格交替构成，在超薄膜（≤5个单层）中稳定，表现出各向异性铁磁性、反常霍尔效应和负磁阻，并可与半导体Ge自然集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用于自旋电子学应用并作为二维磁体前驱体的层状磁体数量仍然很少；作者希望利用相稳定性与维度之间的相互作用，通过相工程设计出新的层状磁体。

Method: 采用相工程策略，利用相稳定性与维度的相互关系，制备了GdAlGe层状多晶型的外延超薄膜（最多5个单层）；通过电子衍射、X射线衍射和电子显微镜表征其结构质量；用磁化测量研究磁性，并用电子输运测量（反常霍尔效应、负磁阻）加以佐证。

Result: 成功制备出由三角形Gd和蜂窝状AlGe晶格交替组成的GdAlGe外延薄膜，超薄膜（≤5单层）结构稳定，更厚的薄膜会出现额外的非期望相；磁化测量显示各向异性铁磁态，输运测量观测到反常霍尔效应和负磁阻，且该材料可自然集成到半导体Ge上。

Conclusion: 本研究证明相工程可用于设计层状磁体，为通过多晶型工程开发层状磁体提供了蓝本，且GdAlGe与Ge半导体的自然集成有望促进实际应用。

Abstract: The recent advances in research on layered magnets have established them as invaluable materials for spintronic applications and precursors to 2D magnets. The problem is that the number of such materials available for experimentation is still low. To tackle the problem, one may employ phase engineering; in particular, the interplay between the phase stability and dimensionality provides great opportunities for design of layered magnets. Here, this interrelation is harnessed to produce epitaxial films of an overlooked layered polymorph of GdAlGe. The compound is formed by alternating triangular Gd and honeycomb AlGe lattices. It is stable in ultrathin films, up to 5 monolayers. In thicker films, an additional unwanted phase emerges. The structural quality of the ultrathin films is witnessed by electron and X-ray diffraction as well as electron microscopy. Magnetization measurements detect an anisotropic ferromagnetic state. The GdAlGe magnetism is corroborated by a study of electron transport revealing the anomalous Hall effect and negative magnetoresistance. The material is naturally integrated with Ge, a technological semiconductor, which should facilitate applications. The present study can serve as a blueprint for design of layered magnets via polymorph engineering.

</details>


### [158] [Atomistic origin and strain control of the finite-temperature dielectric response in BaTiO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07219)
*Ryotaro Sahashi,Po-Yen Chen,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习力场与电场耦合分子动力学，揭示了BaTiO3介电响应的局域原子起源：介电常数随温度的变化由场致Ti-O偏心（off-centering）的角度重分布所决定，而非偏心的幅值或平均极角；温度与双轴应变虽以不同方式改变局域结构，但二者介电响应均可用同一取向描述符统一刻画，为软模行为提供了实空间对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BaTiO3的介电响应随温度强烈变化，但其局域原子尺度的物理起源尚不清楚，需要厘清介电常数与局域原子结构（尤其是Ti-O偏心）之间的定量关系。

Method: 采用耦合电场的分子动力学模拟，结合机器学习力场，系统分析局域Ti-O偏心的幅值、平均极角及场致角度重分布等描述符，并考察温度与双轴应变两种调控方式下介电响应与局域结构的关系。

Result: 介电常数并非由Ti-O偏心的幅值或平均极角决定，而是追随场致局域Ti-O偏心角度的重分布；温度和双轴应变虽对局域结构产生不同影响，但二者的介电响应均遵循同一条以同一取向描述符表述的普适关系。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了以取向描述符为纽带的介电响应统一规律，为BaTiO3的软模行为提供了实空间层面的解释，揭示了局域原子角度重分布这一本征机制。

Abstract: The dielectric response of BaTiO3 (BTO) varies strongly with temperature, yet its local atomic origin remains unclear. Using electric-field-coupled molecular dynamics with a machine-learning force field, we show that the permittivity tracks the field-induced angular redistribution of local Ti-O off-centering rather than its magnitude or mean polar angle. Temperature and biaxial strain modify the local structure differently, but both responses follow a common relation with the same orientational descriptor, providing a real-space counterpart to soft-mode behavior.

</details>


### [159] [Metallic-Phase-Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ Enabled Interface Engineering for Self-Powered and High-Gain WS$_2$ Photodetectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07295)
*Wajid Ali,Ming Huang,Juan Li,Jianhua Huang,Liuli Yang,Sajid Ur Rehman,Chinmay K. Mohanty,Zahir Muhammad,Ziwei Li,Maciej R. Molas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于WS₂/Fe₃GaTe₂范德华异质结的高增益、自供电光电探测器，利用金属性Fe₃GaTe₂作为活性界面接触，实现了零偏压下23.5 A/W的响应度和6.4×10³%的表观外量子效率，以及在-1 V偏压下9.7×10³ A/W的响应度和2.3×10¹³ Jones的比探测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维过渡金属硫族化合物虽具有强的光-物质相互作用，但在光电探测器中存在载流子分离效率低和接触相关损耗的问题，制约了器件性能。作者希望引入金属性Fe₃GaTe₂作为活性界面接触，通过界面工程实现高效载流子分离与提取，从而获得自供电、高灵敏度的二维光电探测器。

Method: 构建WS₂/Fe₃GaTe₂范德华异质结光电探测器，以金属性Fe₃GaTe₂作为活性界面接触。利用功函数失配、界面电荷再分布以及非对称接触几何结构共同形成内建电场，支持零偏压自供电光电探测。器件性能通过不同波长（450、520、633 nm）和偏压条件（0 V、-1 V）下的响应度、外量子效率及噪声比探测率进行表征。

Result: 在450 nm光照、零偏压下，器件获得23.5 A/W的响应度和6.4×10³%的表观外量子效率；在-1 V偏压下，异质结在450、520和633 nm均有光响应，450 nm光照下响应度达9.7×10³ A/W，100 Hz处噪声比探测率为2.3×10¹³ Jones。高光响应归因于界面载流子分离、高效提取以及多层WS₂中陷阱辅助光电导增益的贡献。

Conclusion: Fe₃GaTe₂介导的界面工程是实现自供电、高灵敏度二维光电探测器的有效途径，该WS₂/Fe₃GaTe₂异质结构在可见光范围内展现出优异的响应度和探测能力，为高性能二维光电器件设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Two-dimensional transition-metal dichalcogenides offer strong light-matter interaction but suffer from inefficient carrier separation and contact-related losses in photodetectors. Here, we demonstrate a high-gain WS$_2$/Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ van der Waals heterostructure photodetector, where metallic Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ serves as an active interfacial contact. The work-function mismatch, together with interfacial charge redistribution and asymmetric contact geometry, contributes to a built-in field that supports self-powered photodetection at zero bias. Under 450 nm illumination, the device delivers a zero-bias responsivity of 23.5 A/W and an apparent external quantum efficiency of 6.4 x 10$^3$%. At -1 V biasing, the heterostructure exhibits photoresponse at 450, 520 and 633 nm, achieving a responsivity of 9.7 x 10$^3$ A/W and a noise derived specific detectivity of 2.3 x 10$^13$ Jones at 100 Hz under 450 nm illumination. The high photoresponse is attributed to interfacial carrier separation, efficient extraction, and a likely contribution from trap-assisted photogating in multilayer WS$_2$. These results establish Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$-enabled interface engineering as an effective route for self-powered, highly sensitive 2D photodetectors.

</details>


### [160] [Modular and Cost-effective Scanning Photocurrent Microscopy System for Sub-micron characterization of 2D optoelectronic devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07321)
*Nuria Jiménez-Arévalo,Dan Zheng,Yong Xie,Peng Cheng,Yi Liu,Rafael Luque Merino,Haiwen Yu,Tao Wang,Riccardo Frisenda,Andres Castellanos-Gomez,Qinghua Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于常规金相显微镜改造的自制扫描光电流显微镜（SPCM）平台，通过集成光纤激光器、LED光源、电动XY台、数字相机与电子读出模块，实现光电流与反射强度图的同时采集，达到亚微米分辨率和高度成像保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用扫描光电流显微镜（SPCM）设备价格昂贵、结构复杂，且缺乏灵活性和可扩展性，难以满足2D半导体器件局域光电现象表征的广泛需求。

Method: 作者将传统金相显微镜进行改造，耦合单模光纤激光器与多模光纤高功率LED等不同光源，加装电动XY位移台、数字相机和电子读出模块，从而构建自制SPCM平台；系统可同时采集光电流图与反射强度图，并通过与光学显微照片及AFM形貌数据相互关联以校验成像精度。

Result: 在多种材料（InSe、MoS2、WSe2、石墨烯）和不同衬底（Si/SiO2、光盘）制备的器件上，该平台实现了亚微米空间分辨率和高度成像保真度，光电流图与反射图、光学显微图及AFM数据高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可靠、易获取、可复现的高性能SPCM平台，只需对显微镜做最小改动即可在大多数实验室搭建，用于2D器件的微尺度光电表征。

Abstract: Scanning photocurrent microscopy (SPCM) is a powerful technique for probing local optoelectronic phenomena in 2D semiconducting devices. However, commercial setups remain costly, complex and often lack flexibility and adaptability. In this work, we present a home-built SPCM platform built around the retrofitting of a conventional metallographic microscope by coupling it with different light sources (single-mode fiber-coupled lasers and multimode fiber-coupled high-power LEDs), a motorized XY stage, a digital camera and an electronic readout module. This system enables simultaneous acquisition of photocurrent and reflection intensity maps, requiring minimal modifications of the microscope. We reached sub-micron spatial resolution and high imaging fidelity by correlating photocurrent maps with reflection maps, optical micrographs and AFM topography data on different devices fabricated with different materials (InSe, MoS2, WSe2, Gr), on different substrates (Si/SiO2, compact disk). This work provides a reliable, accessible and reproducible high-performance SPCM platform that can be easily implemented in most laboratories for microscale optoelectronic characterization of 2D devices.

</details>


### [161] [Quantifying thermal and driven magnon populations with femtosecond noise correlation spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07421)
*F. S. Herbst,M. A. Weiss,A. Leitenstorfer,M. Lammel,N. Beaulieu,J. Ben Youssef,R. Schlitz,S. T. B. Goennenwein*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出飞秒噪声关联谱学方法，首次实现对磁子（含热激发非相干磁子与相干激发磁子）在时域上的定量测量，并在铋掺杂钇铁石榴石样品上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确获知磁子总数（包括相干与非相干/热激发磁子）对推进自旋波物理和下一代磁子器件至关重要，但传统频域技术缺乏超快过程所需的时间分辨率，而超快时域方法通常只对相干动力学敏感，无法定量测量非相干磁子。

Method: 采用飞秒噪声关联谱学（femtosecond noise correlation spectroscopy），对自由运行的微波驱动的铋掺杂钇铁石榴石（Bi:YIG）中的热激发和相干磁子模式进行时域定量测量；通过模拟样品的磁子能带结构、磁光响应函数以及实验所用光斑尺寸来建模实验数据并提取磁子数。

Result: 该方法建立了磁子模式计算与实验可测磁性质之间的关联，能够在不同实验条件下高保真地重现磁光关联信号的波形和幅度，成功定量提取了热磁子和相干激发磁子的数目。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性或非平衡条件下磁子模式的光学层析成像开辟了新途径，并可推广到其他凝聚态体系中超快非相干动力学的研究。

Abstract: Precise knowledge of the total number of magnons, including both coherent and incoherent (e.g. thermal) excitations, is imperative for the advancement of fundamental spin-wave physics and the development of next-generation magnonic devices. In particular, quantifying magnons is key to understanding magnon transport phenomena, the nonlinear regime, or ultrafast magnetization dynamics. Typically, incoherent magnons are accessed by frequency-domain techniques, which lack the temporal resolution required for ultrafast processes, while ultrafast time-domain methods are generally sensitive only to the coherent dynamics. In this work, we demonstrate that femtosecond noise correlation spectroscopy enables a fully quantitative, time-domain measurement of both thermal and coherently excited magnon modes in bismuth-substituted yttrium iron garnet driven by a free-running microwave. We model the experimental data and extract the magnon number by simulating the magnon band structure of the sample, the magneto-optical response function, and the optical spot size used in the experiment. Our analysis establishes a connection between magnon mode calculations and experimentally accessible magnetic properties and fiducially reproduces the waveform and amplitude of the magneto-optical correlation signal for different experimental conditions. These results open a new pathway towards the optical tomography of magnon modes in non-linear or non-equilibrium conditions and can be readily extended to study ultrafast incoherent dynamics in other condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [162] [Benchmarking Universal Machine Learning Force Fields for Crystal Structure Prediction of High-Energy Molecular Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07477)
*Musiha Mahfuza Mukta,Osman Goni Ridwan,Romain Perriot,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文评估了通用机器学习原子间势（UMLIPs）在高能分子晶体几何弛豫中的可靠性，发现经典力场（GAFF）预弛豫能为UMLIP弛豫提供更合适的初始结构，从而降低失败率并节省计算成本；其中MACE-OFF在势能面平滑性、结构保真度和应力收敛之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMLIPs为基于几何弛豫和能量排序的分子晶体高通量筛选提供了快速途径，但其在化学多样的含能材料中的可靠性尚不清楚，尤其担心这些UMLIPs在弛豫周期性晶体时会对分子连接性过于敏感而导致断键。

Method: 在一个大型高能分子晶体数据库上，测试了三种模型（MACE、MACE-OFF和UMA）并结合通用AMBER力场（GAFF），检验“经典力场预弛豫能为后续UMLIP弛豫提供更合适初始几何”的假设；通过比较失败率与稳健性分析评估各模型的权衡。

Result: 直接的MACE-OFF和UMA表现出很高的弛豫成功率，且最接近地保持了参考几何，但在少数罕见情况下仍会失败；使用GAFF预弛豫能系统地减少失败弛豫数目并降低计算成本。比较性失败与稳健性分析揭示了各模型之间不同的权衡。

Conclusion: GAFF预弛豫可作为UMLIP弛豫的有效前置步骤，提升成功率并降低成本；在评估的模型中，MACE-OFF在势能面平滑性、结构保真度与应力收敛之间取得更好折衷，适合作为自动化结构优化的可靠基础。

Abstract: Recent developments of universal machine learning interatomic potentials (UMLIPs) offer a fast route for screening molecular crystals based on geometry relaxation and energy ranking, but their reliability across chemically diverse energetic materials remains elusive. In particular, it is unclear whether or not these UMLIPs are over-sensitive to break the desired molecular connectivity for relaxing the periodic crystals. Herein we tested the hypothesis that classical force-field pre-relaxation can provide a more suitable starting geometry for subsequent UMLIP relaxation on a large database of high energy molecular crystals. Three models (MACE, MACE-OFF and UMA) in conjunction with the General Amber Force Field (GAFF) were applied to test this hypothesis. Among them, direct MACE-OFF and UMA showed very high relaxation success and preserved the reference geometries most closely, but they still exhibit failures for some rare cases. Using GAFF pre-relaxation can systematically reduce the number of failed relaxations with lower computational costs. Our comparative failure and robustness analyses revealed distinct trade-offs among the evaluated models. Among them, MACE-OFF achieves a better compromise between potential energy surface smoothness, structural fidelity, and stress convergence, serving as a good choice to provide a reliable foundation for automated structural optimization.

</details>


### [163] [Switchable Altermagnetism in a Layered van der Waals Metal-Organic Framework Driven by Spin-Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07548)
*Diego López-Alcalá,Alberto M. Ruiz,Andrei Shumilin,José J. Baldoví*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用分子材料中的自旋交叉（spin-crossover）作为一种前所未有的、多功能的、可编程的途径，实现交变磁性（altermagnetism）的按需开关。通过密度泛函计算，作者在层状范德华MOF MnX₂(tdz)₂（X = Cl, Br；tdz = 噻二唑）中证实了交变磁基态，并发现静水压力诱导的高自旋→低自旋转变会改变磁基态对称性，从而抑制非相对论自旋劈裂，实现交变磁性的开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性的动态调控是实现其独特自旋相关功能并转化为实用自旋电子学器件的关键，但有效的开关机制尚未被充分探索，亟需提出可行的调控方案。

Method: 作者采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究了层状范德华金属有机框架（MOF）MnX₂(tdz)₂（X = Cl, Br）的磁性与电子结构。通过分析各向异性层间交换相互作用、d波型动量空间自旋劈裂以及自旋分裂输运响应，并在静水压力下考察高自旋→低自旋转变对Mn d轨道占据、磁交换网络及磁基态对称性的影响。

Result: 1）MnX₂(tdz)₂存在由各向异性层间交换相互作用稳定的交变磁基态，呈现特征性的d波型动量空间自旋劈裂和相应的自旋分裂输运响应；2）静水压力诱导的高自旋→低自旋转变重构了Mn的d轨道占据，改变磁交换网络，稳定了一种对称性不同、从而抑制非相对论自旋劈裂的反铁磁基态；3）自旋交叉并非直接改变电子结构，而是通过改变磁基态对称性来开关交变磁性。

Conclusion: 本文确立了分子自旋交叉交变磁体作为外部可重构自旋电子学器件平台的可行性，为按需开关交变磁性提供了一条开创性、通用且可编程的途径。

Abstract: Dynamical control of altermagnetism is a key requirement for translating its unique spin-dependent functionalities into practical spintronic devices, yet effective switching mechanisms remain largely unexplored. Here, we open an unprecedented, versatile and programmable route based on spin-crossover to switch altermagnetism on demand in molecular materials. Using density functional calculations, we demonstrate an altermagnetic ground state in the layered van der Waals MOFs MnX$_2$(tdz)$_2$ (X = Cl, Br; tdz = thiadiazole), stabilized by anisotropic interlayer exchange interactions, which gives rise to a characteristic d-wave momentum-space spin splitting and an associated spin-splitter transport response. Our findings reveal that under hydrostatic pressure, a high-spin to low-spin transition reconfigures the Mn d-orbital occupation, thus modifying the magnetic exchange network, and stabilizing a different antiferromagnetic ground state whose symmetry suppresses the nonrelativistic spin splitting. Crucially, spin-crossover switches altermagnetism not by directly altering the electronic structure, but by changing the symmetry of the magnetic ground state. These results establish molecular spin-crossover altermagnets as a platform for externally reconfigurable spintronic devices.

</details>


### [164] [Exciton Coherence in CsPbBr3 Nanocrystals is Bounded by Phonon-Mediated Bright-Triplet Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07739)
*Tara Šverko,Annette J. Jones,Chantalle J. Krajewska,Peter C. Sercel,Alexander L. Efros,Alexander E. Kaplan,Niamh L. Brown,Stefano Toso,Moungi G. Bawendi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究了 CsPbBr3 钙钛矿纳米晶中声学声子介导的激子精细结构弛豫过程，并证明该过程是限制单光子源相干性的内在因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的不可区分单光子和纠缠光子对源是量子光子技术的基础，胶体卤化铅钙钛矿纳米晶是极具潜力的光源，但其激子相干性质尚未被充分理解，需要查明限制其相干性的物理机制。

Method: 在 4 K 低温下对单个 CsPbBr3 纳米晶进行光谱测量，研究声学声子介导的激子精细结构弛豫（EFSR）如何导致亮激子三重态之间的布居泄漏，并定量测量该泄漏速率与辐射寿命的比值及其对相干性的影响。

Result: EFSR 驱动的布居泄漏速率达到辐射速率的 0.64 ± 0.04 倍，使 4 K 下的态相干性被限制在变换极限的 0.81 倍；且该泄漏途径是纳米晶的内在性质，与周围环境无关。

Conclusion: 精细结构工程和声学声子工程是提升钙钛矿量子光源相干性的有效内在途径，为材料和器件设计提供了明确目标。

Abstract: Scalable sources of indistinguishable single photons or entangled photon pairs are fundamental to many quantum photonic technologies. Colloidal lead halide perovskite nanocrystals are promising such sources, but their exciton coherent properties are not fully understood. We show that in single CsPbBr3 nanocrystals at 4 K, acoustic phonon-mediated exciton fine structure relaxation (EFSR) drives leakage of population between the bright exciton triplet states, competing with the radiative lifetime. The leakage pathway reaches 0.64 $\pm$ 0.04 of the radiative rate, bounding state coherence to 0.81 times the transform limit at 4 K. This pathway is intrinsic to the nanocrystal, not its environment, making it a tractable target for materials and device design. We identify fine structure and acoustic phonon engineering as effective intrinsic routes to higher coherence in perovskite quantum light sources.

</details>


### [165] [High-Field Electron Transport in AlGaN alloys: A Full-Band Monte Carlo Study Based on Ab Initio Supercell Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07773)
*Animesh Datta,Matinehsadat Hosseinigheidari,Uttam Singisetti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用基于超胞的全能带蒙特卡罗方法研究 AlGaN 合金的高场电子输运特性，计算了速度-电场特性、散射机制、瞬态速度过冲及温度依赖性，为 AlGaN 基射频和功率器件设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlGaN 合金是面向下一代功率和射频电子应用的有前景的宽/超宽带隙半导体。为充分发挥 AlGaN 器件的潜力，需要准确理解电子输运过程，以预测器件性能并识别材料在各种工作条件下的极限。

Method: 采用基于超胞（supercell）的全能带蒙特卡罗（full band Monte Carlo）方法。超胞方法可显式捕捉合金体系的真实无序性，从而更真实地描述载流子输运。研究跨越不同 Al 组分，计算速度-电场特性，并详细分析各散射机制的作用；同时考察不同 Al 组分下的瞬态电子动力学以研究速度过冲行为，并研究超宽禁带 Al0.75Ga0.25N 速度-电场特性的温度依赖性。

Result: 给出了不同 Al 组分下的峰值速度、饱和速度和临界电场等关键输运指标；阐明了各散射机制对高场输运的贡献；获得了不同 Al 组分下的速度过冲行为，以及 Al0.75Ga0.25N 在高温条件下的输运性能。

Conclusion: 研究提供了对 AlGaN 合金高场输运的深入理解，并为 AlGaN 基射频与功率电子器件的设计提供了指导。

Abstract: AlGaN alloys are promising wide and ultra-wide-bandgap semiconductors for next-generation power and RF electronics applications. To realize the full potential of AlGaN based devices, it is important to understand the electron transport to accurately predict device performance and identify material limits under various operating conditions. In this work, the high-field electron transport properties of AlxGa1-xN are investigated using a supercell based full band Monte Carlo method. The supercell approach is employed to explicitly capture the true disorder of the alloy system, enabling a more realistic description of carrier transport. The velocity field characteristics are calculated across a range of Al compositions to evaluate key transport metrics, including peak velocity, saturation velocity, and critical electric field. The role of different scattering mechanisms is studied in detail to understand the high field transport mechanism in the AlGaN alloy system. In addition to steady state transport, transient electron dynamics are examined for various Al fractions to study velocity-overshoot behavior, which is especially important for improving the performance of scaled RF devices. Finally, the temperature dependence of the velocity field characteristics in ultra-wide-bandgap Al0.75Ga0.25N is investigated to assess its transport performance under high temperature conditions. These results provide a detailed understanding of high-field transport in AlGaN alloys and offer guidance for the design of AlGaN-based RF and power electronic devices.

</details>


### [166] [Temperature-Dependent nonlinear optics from first-principles: Second-Harmonic Generation in few-layers MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07796)
*Anna Romani,Claudio Attaccalite,Myrta Grüning*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种第一性原理实时（real-time）方法，研究固体在有限温度下的非线性光学响应，重点计算单层与三层MoS₂的二次谐波产生（SHG）及其非平庸的温度依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对固体非线性响应的第一性原理研究多局限于零温或唯象处理温度效应，缺乏将电子-声子耦合引起的准粒子重整化与退相干定量纳入实时动力学的框架。实验上观察到单层MoS₂的SHG强度随温度升高而增强，亟需从微观机制上加以解释。

Method: 作者采用第一性原理实时方法：1）利用密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）计算晶格动力学与电子-声子矩阵元；2）由电子自能的Fan项和Debye-Waller项计算准粒子能量重整化与寿命；3）将温度效应以准粒子能量重整化及正比于准粒子寿命的退相干项引入实时演化；4）电子激发采用独立粒子近似，并应用该方法计算单层与三层MoS₂的二次谐波产生。

Result: 研究发现SHG表现出非平庸的温度依赖性，其根源在于强烈的晶格动量依赖的准粒子能量重整化。声子模式分析表明，单层MoS₂中声学模式、三层MoS₂中剪切模式分别决定了整体的晶格动量依赖关系。这一非平庸温度依赖性有助于解释实验上单层MoS₂在特定激光能量下SHG强度随温度升高的现象。

Conclusion: 该第一性原理实时方法能够定量描述有限温度下固体的非线性响应，成功再现并合理解释了MoS₂中SHG的温度行为，为研究电子-声子耦合对非线性光学过程的调控机制提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We present a first-principles real-time approach to study non-linear response of solids at finite temperature. Finite temperature effects are included as renormalization of the quasiparticle energies and a dephasing term proportional to the quasiparticle lifetimes. We evaluate electron-phonon matrix elements from Density-Functional Perturbation Theory for lattice dynamics and then calculate quasiparticles renormalization and lifetime from the Fan and Debye-Waller terms of the electron self-energy. Electron excitations are treated at the independent particle level of approximation. We apply the approach to the second-harmonic generation (SHG) in monolayer and trilayer MoS$_2$. We observe a nontrivial temperature-dependence of the SHG due to a strong crystal-momentum dependent quasiparticle renormalization. From the phonon-mode analysis we find that the coupling with acoustic and shear modes determines the overall crystal-momentum dependence respectively in monolayer and trilayer MoS$_2$. The nontrivial temperature-dependence of the SHG can help rationalize the increase of SHG intensity with increasing temperature observed in monolayer MoS$_2$ at a given laser energy [Adv. Optical Mater. 8, 2000441 (2020)].

</details>


### [167] [Atomistic modeling of molecular beam epitaxy growth of SrTiO3 and Sr2TiO4 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08114)
*Guangfu Luo,Dane Morgan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理计算揭示了SrTiO3与Sr2TiO4钙钛矿薄膜MBE生长中的三种意外生长机制：氧空位动态加速扩散、TiSr缺陷促进SrO岛形成，以及Ti/TiO2插入SrO双层导致异常生长序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBE虽以原子层精度著称，但意外生长机制可能破坏这一精度。为深入理解并控制钙钛矿氧化物薄膜的MBE生长过程，需要从原子尺度阐明气相分子种类、表面反应与扩散动力学。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，系统研究SrTiO3衬底上SrTiO3和Ruddlesden-Popper相Sr2TiO4薄膜MBE生长中可能的气相分子种类、其在薄膜表面和层边缘的反应与扩散动力学。

Result: 发现三种关键机制：(1) 无缺陷衬底表面扩散过程中可动态诱导氧空位，显著加速扩散；(2) SrO层虽预期以单层模式生长，但TiSr缺陷可能促进SrO岛的形成；(3) 吸附在SrO双层上的Ti原子与TiO2分子可插入SrO双层，导致意外生长序列。

Conclusion: 这些发现对金属氧化物薄膜的MBE生长具有更广泛的意义，并为实现生长过程的高精度控制提供了指导。

Abstract: Molecular beam epitaxy (MBE) is renowned for its potential for atomic layer control, but unexpected growth mechanisms can potentially compromise this level of precision. In this study, we employ first-principles calculations to investigate the atomistic processes governing the MBE growth of perovskite SrTiO3 and Ruddlesden-Popper Sr2TiO4 films on a SrTiO3 substrate. We systematically explore the potential molecular species in the gas phase and their reactions and diffusion dynamics on the film surfaces and layer edges. Our analyses uncover three mechanisms of importance for understanding this type of growth. First, oxygen vacancies can be dynamically induced during surface diffusion on defect-free substrate and noticeably accelerate the diffusion processes. Second, while the SrO layer is expected to grow in a single-layer growth mode, the presence of potential TiSr defects may promote the formation of SrO islands. Lastly, adsorbed Ti atoms and TiO2 molecules on the SrO bilayer can insert into SrO bilayers, resulting in an unexpected growth sequence. These findings may have broader implications for the MBE growth of metal oxide films and provide guidance for achieving improved control over their growth processes.

</details>


### [168] [Pressure Evolution of Atomic Volume Systematics in Transition Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08197)
*Masaaki Geshi,Yuichi Akahama*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论计算，研究了过渡金属原子体积-原子序数抛物线关系在高达400 GPa极端压缩下的演化，发现环境压力下的抛物线趋势在高压下转变为特征的三次方型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究过渡金属中广为人知的原子体积随原子序数呈抛物线依赖关系在极端压缩条件下（高达400 GPa）如何演化，以揭示其物理机制和与实验、先前计算的关联。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）第一性原理计算，对过渡金属在0-400 GPa压力范围内的原子体积与原子序数的关系进行系统性模拟分析。

Result: 结果显示，环境压力下的抛物线趋势在高压下演变为特征的三次方（cubic-like）型行为；该演化归因于bcc结构过渡金属具有更高的可压缩性，并伴随总能量相对较大的增加。

Conclusion: 过渡金属原子体积-原子序数的抛物线关系在极端压缩下转变为三次方型行为，其机制与bcc过渡金属较高的可压缩性及总能量增幅相关，研究结果与先前实验观测和第一性原理计算具有关联性。

Abstract: We investigated the evolution of the well-known parabolic dependence of atomic volume on atomic number in transition metals under extreme compression at pressures up to 400 GPa using density functional theory calculations. Our results reveal that the ambient-pressure parabolic trend transforms into a characteristic cubic-like behavior at high pressures. This evolution is attributed to the higher compressibility of bcc transition metals associated with comparatively large increases in the total energy. The present findings are discussed in relation to previous experimental observations and first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [169] [Light-facilitated ferroelectric switching in wurtzite crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08270)
*Ricardo Jiménez-Sánchez,Fernando Salazar,Miguel Cruz-Irisson,Riccardo Rurali,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无电场的光学策略，通过超带隙光激发产生的载流子显著降低纤锌矿铁电体（以LaN为代表）的极化反转能垒，从而解决其矫顽场过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤锌矿铁电体兼具大剩余极化和CMOS兼容性，是下一代非易失性存储器的理想平台，但其本征矫顽场很大，源于极性纤锌矿相与非极性六方相之间的高能垒，阻碍了实际应用。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，以LaN为代表性纤锌矿铁电体，研究超带隙电子光激发产生的光生载流子对六方中间相与纤锌矿基态能量差的影响，并考察其对铁电极化反转能垒的降低作用，同时在岩盐多晶型中验证该机制的普适性。

Result: 光生载流子显著降低了六方中间相与纤锌矿基态之间的能量差，使铁电极化反转能垒大幅降低。该效应源于光激发引起的极性相部分金属化，屏蔽了稳定铁电序的偶极-偶极相互作用，从而有利于竞争的非极性结构；该机制在岩盐多晶型中也得到进一步证实。

Conclusion: 光可作为一种强大且非侵入性的手段来调控纤锌矿铁电体的极化反转，这为更快、更低电压且更节能的非易失性存储器技术开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Wurtzite ferroelectrics combine large remanent polarization with full CMOS compatibility, positioning them as a leading platform for next-generation non-volatile memory. Their practical deployment, however, is hindered by an intrinsically large coercive field, rooted in the high energy barrier separating the polar wurtzite phase from the nonpolar hexagonal phase that mediates polarization switching. Here, using first-principles calculations, we propose an alternative, field-free strategy for lowering this barrier: above-bandgap electronic photoexcitation. Taking LaN as a representative wurtzite ferroelectric, we show that light induced carriers dramatically reduce the energy difference between the hexagonal intermediate phase and the wurtzite ground state, sharply reducing the energy barrier to ferroelectric switching. This effect originates from a photoinduced partial metallization of the polar phase, which screens the dipole-dipole interactions that stabilize ferroelectric order and thereby favors the competing nonpolar structure. The robustness of this mechanism is further confirmed for the rocksalt polymorph. Our results establish light as a powerful, non-invasive route to controlling ferroelectric switching in wurtzites, opening a path toward faster, lower-voltage, and more energy-efficient non-volatile memory technologies.

</details>


### [170] [Flexoelectric Polarization in Wrinkled Janus Transition-Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08295)
*Stefan Velja,Surender Kumar,Domenico Corona,Caterina Cocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT计算结合连续弹性模型，系统研究了褶皱Janus MoSSe/MoSeS单层的电子与挠曲电响应，发现全局面外极化随纳米褶皱投影纵横比呈线性变化，并据此提出仅含有效二维挠曲电系数的几何模型，可准确预测偏振而无需高阶非线性参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用面外褶皱的应变梯度工程是调控二维材料电子与机电性质的有效途径，但此前对褶皱Janus单层中挠曲电响应的微观机制及简单预测模型尚不清晰，需建立明确的几何设计规则以指导柔性纳米电子与光电子应用。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，结合连续弹性（elastica）建模，系统扫描不同褶皱尺寸与压缩应变水平（5%–20%），并通过原子与轨道分辨的电荷密度分析揭示极化来源。

Result: 全局面外极化以纳米褶皱投影纵横比为参数呈线性行为；开发出含有效二维挠曲电系数的物理几何模型，无需高阶非线性参数即可准确预测DFT极化；中心Mo 4d轨道流形充当稳健的线性挠曲电核心，局部曲率驱动S 3p与Se 4p亚层流形间连续、化学不对称的电荷转移。

Conclusion: 研究确立了利用Janus材料挠曲电性的清晰几何设计规则，为柔性纳米电子学和光电子学中调控和使用挠曲电效应提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Strain-gradient engineering via out-of-plane wrinkling offers a powerful route to tune electronic and electromechanical properties in two-dimensional (2D) materials. Here, we systematically investigate the electronic and flexoelectric response of wrinkled Janus MoSSe/MoSeS monolayers using density functional theory (DFT) calculations coupled with continuum elastica modeling. Exploring varying wrinkle sizes and compressive strain levels ($5\%-20\%$), we show that the global out-of-plane polarization follows a linear behavior when parameterized by the projected aspect ratio of the nanowrinkles. On this basis, we develop a physically grounded geometric model incorporating an effective 2D flexoelectric coefficient, which accurately predicts DFT polarizations without requiring higher-order nonlinear parameters. Atom- and orbital-resolved charge density analyses reveal the microscopic origin of this behavior: while the central Mo $4d$-orbital manifold acts as a robust, linear flexoelectric core, local curvature drives continuous, chemically asymmetric charge transfer between the S $3p$ and Se $4p$ sublayer manifolds. Our findings establish clear geometric design rules for harnessing Janus-based flexoelectricity for flexible nanoelectronics and optoelectronics.

</details>


### [171] [Band Structure Modulation of ZrO2 Nanoparticles for Control of CO Adsorption Properties: A Combined Density Functional Theory - Density Functional Tight Binding Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08329)
*Kexin Chen,William Dawson,Aulia Sukma Hutama,Takahito Nakajima,Keisuke Kameda,Manabu Ihara,Sergei Manzhos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一种混合 DFTB-力场（DFTB-FF）框架，用于研究几纳米尺寸的氧化锆（ZrO2）纳米颗粒及其对 CO 分子的吸附，揭示了化学计量比和低配位表面位点对电子结构与反应活性的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFTB 方法虽能高效处理大体系，但其传统的排斥势在复杂氧化物纳米结构的几何优化和吸附性质描述上精度不足、优化鲁棒性差。作者旨在通过引入经典长程原子间势和配位依赖的 Zr-C 势，扩展 DFTB 对氧化锆纳米颗粒及其 CO 吸附的适用性，并阐明纳米结构化和化学计量比对吸附活性的影响机制。

Method: 结合 DFT 与 DFTB 计算，开发混合 DFTB-FF 框架：能带结构计算沿用现有 Slater-Koster 方案，同时用经典长程原子间势替代传统排斥势以提高结构优化与吸附计算精度，并引入配位依赖的 Zr-C 势以区分类体相晶面位点与欠配位尖端/边缘位点的局域化学环境。基于该框架对几纳米、不同化学计量比的氧化锆纳米颗粒及其 CO 吸附进行模拟。

Result: 混合 DFTB-FF 方法显著提升了几何优化的鲁棒性。计算表明，可通过终止端化学计量比调控得到本征、p 型或 n 型电子结构，从而调节氧化锆纳米颗粒的吸附活性。化学计量比的纳米颗粒不活化 C-O 键，而富 Zr（n 型）纳米颗粒中的低配位位点表现出化学吸附，电荷向 CO 反键 LUMO 衍生轨道转移，导致 C-O 键活化。

Conclusion: 化学计量比调控的电子结构与纳米结构化引入的欠配位表面位点，共同决定了氧化锆纳米颗粒对 CO 的吸附强度与反应活性，为复杂氧化物纳米结构的 DFTB 模拟提供了实用策略。

Abstract: We present a combined density functional theory (DFT) and density functional tight binding (DFTB) study of zirconia (ZrO2) nanoparticles of experimentally relevant sizes of several nanometers and their interactions with the CO molecule. A hybrid DFTB - Force Field (DFTB-FF) framework is developed, whereby band structure calculations rely on an existing Slater-Koster framework, while the accuracy of structural optimization and adsorption properties is controlled by the introduction of classical long-range interatomic potentials into DFTB instead of the traditional repulsive potentials. Additionally, coordination-dependent Zr-C potentials are introduced to account for the distinct local chemical environments of bulk-like facet sites and under-coordinated tip and edge sites, thereby improving the description of CO adsorption.
